`CHANGELOG.md`:

```md
# Changelog

## 0.1.0

- Transparent, click-through crosshair overlay for Windows, X11, and Wayland
  (wlr-layer-shell with XWayland fallback).
- 100 built-in tactical and professional crosshairs, ADS/fire behaviour, and
  profile-driven weapon-slot switching.
- Event-driven tray daemon with on-demand X-Hair Studio (library, designer,
  profiles, community browser) that exits and releases resources when closed.
- Portable `XHAIR1:` share codes, JSON Schema validation, PNG/SVG rendering, and
  the headless `xhair-cli` validate/build-index tooling.
- GitHub-hosted community catalog with ETag caching, SHA-256 integrity checks,
  and an issue-based submission flow.
- XDG/Windows Run autostart, single-instance locking, and a simple X logo.

```

`README.md`:

```md
<p align="center">
  <img src="assets/x-hair-logo.svg" width="112" alt="X-Hair logo">
</p>

# X-Hair

**An ultra-lightweight, anti-cheat-safe, cross-platform crosshair overlay written in pure Rust.**

X-Hair draws a transparent, click-through overlay at the center of your screen. It ships with 100
hand-tuned professional and tactical reticles, per-game weapon-slot switching driven by OS-native
input events, a community catalog hosted entirely on GitHub, and a companion designer that is
spawned on demand and fully destroyed when closed.

No Electron. No WebView. No DLL injection. No game memory access. No hooks. No render loop.

```
      \\   //
       \\ //
        \X     X-Hair
        /X     crosshair overlay
       // \\
      //   \\
```

The project logo (`assets/x-hair-logo.svg`) and the tray icon are the same simple X mark.
The tray icon is generated at runtime as a transparent 32 px bitmap; there are no photographic
or third-party image assets in this repository.

---

## Why it is safe

X-Hair is engineered to be **anti-cheat safe by construction**:

| Concern | X-Hair behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Game memory | Never read or written |
| Process injection / DLL loading | Never performed |
| Graphics API hooks (D3D/Vulkan/OpenGL) | None |
| Overlay mechanism | OS-native transparent, click-through window |
| Weapon switching | OS-native passive input listeners (Raw Input / evdev) |
| Network | Only outbound HTTPS `GET` of the public community `index.json` |
| Telemetry | None |

Passive global input is read through documented Windows Raw Input (`RIDEV_INPUTSINK`) and Linux
`evdev`. On Linux, if the process cannot open `/dev/input/event*`, X-Hair keeps running in static
overlay mode and shows the exact one-line fix in the tray/studio notification area.

> Competitive integrity: crosshair overlays are permitted by many titles but not all. You are
> responsible for following each game's terms of service. X-Hair never interacts with the game.

---

## Install

### Windows 10 / 11

1. Download `x-hair-windows-x86_64.zip` from the Releases page and extract `x-hair.exe`.
2. Run it once. X-Hair starts in the system tray and shows your active crosshair.
3. Press **Alt + Shift + X** to toggle the overlay.

### Linux

Prebuilt Linux binaries are published as `x-hair-linux-x86_64.tar.gz`; you can also build from
source with the instructions below.

For global weapon-slot switching, give your user permission to read input devices:

```bash
sudo usermod -aG input "$USER"
# log out and back in (or reboot) for the group change to apply
```

Or install the provided udev rule instead of changing groups:

```bash
echo 'KERNEL=="event*", SUBSYSTEM=="input", MODE="0660", GROUP="input"' \
  | sudo tee /etc/udev/rules.d/70-x-hair.rules
sudo udevadm control --reload-rules && sudo udevadm trigger
```

Without these permissions X-Hair still works: it simply stays on the static overlay and tells you
what to run.

Wayland is supported through `zwlr_layer_shell_v1` (wlroots, KDE Plasma, Sway, Hyprland, river).
Compositors without layer-shell fall back to X11/XWayland.

### Start with OS

Toggle **Start with OS** in the tray menu. On Linux this writes an XDG autostart entry; on Windows
it writes the current-user `Run` registry value.

---

## Using X-Hair

* **Tray → Toggle Overlay** (Alt + Shift + X)
* **Tray → Active Profile**: Fortnite, Valorant, CS2, Apex presets are pre-configured
* **Tray → Quick Presets**: favorites and recently used reticles
* **Tray → Open X-Hair Studio**: the on-demand designer/library/profiles/community window
* **Tray → Sync Community Hub**: refresh the GitHub-hosted catalog

The Studio window is created only when requested and dropped (all window, GPU, and font memory
released) when closed. The background overlay is event-driven: it repaints only when the active
crosshair, visibility, ADS, bloom, or weapon slot actually changes.

### Default profiles

| Game | Slot 1 | Slot 2 | Slot 3 | Slot 4 | Slot 5 | ADS (RMB hold) | Fire |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Fortnite Tactical | ADS micro-dot | Bugha AR | Tactical shotgun ring | Tactical SMG ring | Sniper mil-dot | ADS micro-dot | Bloom |
| Valorant | TenZ | ScreaM | hidden | — | — | unchanged | none |
| CS2 | s1mple | NiKo | hidden | — | — | unchanged | none |
| Apex Legends | ImperialHal | ImperialHal | — | Shotgun (Mouse4) | — | hide crosshair | none |

Edit any of these in **Studio → Tactical Profiles**.

---

## Design your own crosshair with AI

A crosshair is just a small JSON document, so you can describe the look you want to any AI
assistant and have it generate a spec for you. No code changes are required. Treat the result as
untrusted input: validate it locally before importing, and only paste JSON — never run commands,
links, or files an AI suggests for this project.

### 1. Paste this prompt into your AI

```text
You are an expert FPS crosshair designer. Create one X-Hair crosshair as JSON.

I will paste the official JSON Schema after these instructions. Follow it exactly.

Output rules:
- Return ONLY one JSON object. No markdown fences, no commentary.
- Include every required field and do not add fields the schema does not allow.
- schema_version must be 1.
- id must be lowercase kebab-case, unique, 2-64 characters.
- Use 1-4 layers, ordered back-to-front (max 8).
- Colors are {"r":0-255,"g":0-255,"b":0-255,"a":0-255}; use alpha 200-255 for the
  main shape and 120-220 for a dark outline.
- canvas_size 64-128. Keep offset_x and offset_y at 0 unless I ask otherwise.
- global_opacity between 0.85 and 1.0.
- Keep the center readable: gap >= 1 px, no solid blob wider than ~6 px.
- Add a dark outline (0.5-1.5 px) or a second dark layer so the reticle works on
  both bright and dark scenes.
- Set "bloom": null unless I explicitly ask for fire bloom.
- Do not invent share codes, URLs, file paths, or shell commands. JSON only.

Design brief:
- Game: [Valorant / CS2 / Fortnite / Apex / Warzone / R6 / Overwatch 2 / universal]
- Playstyle: [e.g. precise tap-fire, aggressive SMG, sniper]
- Shape: [e.g. small cyan dot with four short ticks, segmented ring, brackets]
- Color: [e.g. cyan, white with black outline, magenta]

Use the file community/crosshairs/valorant/valorant-community-cyan-dot.json as a
style and formatting reference if I paste it too.
```

Then paste the contents of [`community/schema/crosshair.schema.json`](community/schema/crosshair.schema.json)
(or the reference JSON file above) after the prompt. The schema is the source of truth for field
names, enum values, and ranges. ADS and fire behaviour are configured in tactical profiles, not in
the crosshair JSON, so ask the AI only for the reticle itself.

### 2. Save, validate, and preview

Save the AI reply exactly as-is to `my-crosshair.json`, then check it:

```bash
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- validate my-crosshair.json
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- render-png my-crosshair.json preview.png
```

Open `preview.png`. If the validator reports an unknown field or an out-of-range number, paste the
error back to the AI and ask for a corrected full JSON object.

### 3. Import it into X-Hair

Either generate a share code and paste it into **Studio → Designer → Import / export**:

```bash
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- encode my-crosshair.json
```

Or copy the file into the user crosshair directory using the `id` as the file name, then reopen
Studio:

```bash
# Linux
mkdir -p ~/.config/xhair/X-Hair/crosshairs
cp my-crosshair.json ~/.config/xhair/X-Hair/crosshairs/my-crosshair.json
```

```powershell
# Windows
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force "$env:APPDATA\xhair\X-Hair\crosshairs" | Out-Null
Copy-Item my-crosshair.json "$env:APPDATA\xhair\X-Hair\crosshairs\my-crosshair.json"
```

Replace `my-crosshair` with the `id` from the JSON; the file name must match it exactly.

Use **Apply** in the Library tab, or load it into the Designer to keep tuning it. To share it with
everyone, follow [community/CONTRIBUTING.md](community/CONTRIBUTING.md).

### 4. Iterate

Ask follow-ups such as “make the gap 1 px larger, use a 1 px black outline, and remove the
outer ring” and require the AI to return the complete JSON again. Small numeric changes are easier
to review than a full redesign, and `preview.png` lets you compare versions side by side.

---

## Repository layout

```
crates/
  xhair-core/       schema, share codes, 100 built-in presets, profiles, config, validator
  xhair-render/     pure-Rust tiny-skia vector rasterizer, PNG and SVG exporters
  xhair-platform/   Win32 / X11 / Wayland overlays, raw input, active-window detection
  xhair-community/  GitHub-hosted catalog sync and submission helpers
  xhair-app/        tray daemon, weapon-slot FSM, on-demand egui Studio
  xhair-cli/        headless validate / render-png / build-index / encode / decode
community/          JSON Schema, contributed crosshairs, contribution guide
assets/             the simple X logo (SVG and PNG)
.github/            issue form, PR template, release and community-hub workflows
```

---

## Building from source

Requirements: Rust 1.90+ and a C toolchain.

### Linux

```bash
# Build dependencies for the Studio + tray (Debian/Ubuntu names)
sudo apt install build-essential pkg-config libgtk-3-dev libayatana-appindicator3-dev \
                 libxkbcommon-dev libwayland-dev libx11-dev libxcb1-dev

cargo build --release -p xhair-app --target x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
# binary: target/x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu/release/x-hair
```

### Windows

```powershell
rustup target add x86_64-pc-windows-msvc
cargo build --release -p xhair-app --target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc
# binary: target\x86_64-pc-windows-msvc\release\x-hair.exe
```

### Verify everything

```bash
cargo check --workspace
cargo test --workspace
cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
```

### CLI

```bash
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- validate community/crosshairs
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- render-png community/crosshairs/valorant/valorant-community-cyan-dot.json out.png
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- build-index community/crosshairs dist
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- encode community/crosshairs/valorant/valorant-community-cyan-dot.json
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- decode 'XHAIR1:...' out.json
```

---

## Sharing and the Community Hub

A crosshair is a portable JSON document. X-Hair also packs the same JSON into a compact share code:

```
XHAIR1:<base64url(deflate(json))>
```

Paste a share code into **Studio → Designer → Import / export**, or submit it to the community with
**Studio → Community → Submission page**.

### Contributing (no code required)

1. Open the repository's **Issues → New issue → Submit a crosshair** form.
2. Paste your `XHAIR1:…` share code and pick the game.
3. CI validates the spec, renders a 128×128 PNG preview, and comments on the issue. A maintainer
   merges it into `community/crosshairs/<game>/<id>.json`.

### Contributing (pull request)

```bash
cp my-crosshair.json community/crosshairs/valorant/my-crosshair.json
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- validate community/crosshairs/valorant/my-crosshair.json
git checkout -b crosshair/my-crosshair
git add community/crosshairs/valorant/my-crosshair.json
git commit -m "community: add my-crosshair (Valorant)"
```

After merge, `.github/workflows/community-hub.yml` validates every file, renders previews into
`dist/previews/`, regenerates `dist/index.json`, and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The in-app
Community browser reads that index with ETag caching, so it works offline after the first sync.

See [community/CONTRIBUTING.md](community/CONTRIBUTING.md) for the full guide and the JSON Schema
at [community/schema/crosshair.schema.json](community/schema/crosshair.schema.json).

---

## Configuration

Config and cache live in the platform config directory:

* Windows: `%APPDATA%\xhair\X-Hair\config.toml`
* Linux: `~/.config/xhair/X-Hair/config.toml`

You can edit it by hand; unknown fields are rejected so typos fail loudly.

## License

Dual-licensed under [MIT](LICENSE-MIT) or [Apache-2.0](LICENSE-APACHE), at your option.

```

`SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Security policy

## Reporting a vulnerability

Open a private security advisory at
<https://github.com/ybreakless/x-hair/security/advisories/new>. Please do not file a public issue
for memory-safety, privilege, or remote-input problems. You can expect an initial response within
seven days.

## Threat model

X-Hair is a local desktop overlay. It does not read or write game memory, inject code, hook
graphics APIs, or execute downloaded content. The relevant attack surfaces are:

1. Untrusted community JSON and share codes.
2. Files written under the user's config, cache, and autostart directories.
3. Native OS APIs used by the overlay, input listeners, tray, and autostart code.
4. The single outbound HTTPS request used to fetch the community index.

## Controls

- **Community data is data, not code.** Share codes are deflate-compressed JSON, decoded with a
  size limit and validated by `xhair_core::validate_spec` before use. Catalog entries carry a
  SHA-256 over canonical JSON and the app verifies it before import.
- **Index download limits.** The catalog request has 8 s connect / 15 s read timeouts and an
  8 MiB body cap; catalogs over 10,000 entries or with duplicate ids are rejected.
- **Paths.** Catalog preview paths and crosshair ids are restricted to safe relative slugs.
  User crosshairs are stored as `<safe-id>.json` under the app config directory. No catalog field
  can select an arbitrary filesystem path.
- **No SQL, shell, or eval.** The only process launches are reopening the current executable for
  the Studio and the platform URL opener (`xdg-open` / `explorer`) with a URL built from the fixed
  GitHub repository and percent-encoded parameters. The URL opener refuses schemes other than
  `https://github.com/`.
- **Writes are user-scoped.** Config, cache, autostart, and crosshair writes stay in the current
  user's directories. Config and community-cache writes use a temporary file followed by a
  rename; Wayland shared-memory files are created with `create_new` and unlinked immediately.
- **No secrets.** The project reads no tokens, passwords, or environment credentials. The release
  workflows only use the ephemeral `GITHUB_TOKEN`.

## Native code

`unsafe` is limited to platform bindings: `windows` Win32 calls, `x11rb` / XFixes, Wayland
protocol objects, GTK/muda/tray-icon calls, and the Linux evdev and Windows Raw Input readers.
Each block is kept small and scoped; input buffers are length-checked against the OS-reported
size, and window handles are closed on every path.

## Dependency audit

Run:

```bash
cargo audit
cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
cargo test --workspace
```

As of the `v0.1.0` release, `cargo audit` reports **zero vulnerabilities** and two informational
warnings:

| Advisory | Crate | Assessment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| RUSTSEC-2024-0429 | `glib 0.18.5` | Unsound iterator impls for `glib::VariantStrIter`. X-Hair does not use that type; the crate arrives through GTK 3 tray support. Upstream fix is glib 0.20, blocked by the GTK 3 binding used by `muda`. |
| RUSTSEC-2024-0370 | `proc-macro-error 1.0.4` | Unmaintained build-time proc macro; not present in the shipped binary. |

Both are tracked here rather than silenced. Re-run `cargo audit` after dependency updates.

```

`community/CONTRIBUTING.md`:

```md
# Contributing a crosshair

Community crosshairs are plain JSON documents committed under `community/crosshairs/`.
You can contribute through an issue or a pull request; both paths are validated by CI.

## Rules

- Create the work yourself, or have permission from the creator.
- License the submitted crosshair under **CC0-1.0** so the catalog can redistribute it.
- One crosshair per file.
- The `id` must be lowercase kebab-case, unique across the catalog, and match the file name.
- Set `category` to `community` unless the crosshair is a tactical preset for a specific slot.
- Do not include game files, screenshots, DLLs, or any executable content.

## Submit through an issue (no Git required)

1. Export the crosshair from **X-Hair Studio → Designer → Import / export** as an `XHAIR1:` code.
2. Open **Issues → New issue → Submit a crosshair**.
3. Paste the code, pick the game, and confirm the license checkbox.
4. CI validates the spec and renders a preview; a maintainer merges the generated JSON.

## Submit a pull request

```bash
# 1. Copy an existing file as a template
cp community/crosshairs/valorant/valorant-community-cyan-dot.json \
   community/crosshairs/valorant/my-crosshair.json

# 2. Edit the id, name, author, tags, and layers. The id must match the file name.

# 3. Validate and generate the catalog locally
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- validate community/crosshairs/valorant/my-crosshair.json
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- render-png community/crosshairs/valorant/my-crosshair.json preview.png
cargo run -p xhair-cli -- build-index community/crosshairs dist

# 4. Open a pull request
```

After merge, `.github/workflows/community-hub.yml` rebuilds previews and the index and publishes
them to GitHub Pages. The Studio downloads that index with ETag caching.

## Schema

The authoritative schema is `community/schema/crosshair.schema.json`. The running validator is
`xhair_core::validate_spec`; it is stricter than raw JSON Schema where safety matters (layer count,
canvas bounds, colour and opacity ranges, and id syntax).

## Review checklist

- [ ] `cargo run -p xhair-cli -- validate community/crosshairs` passes.
- [ ] The id is unique and matches the file name.
- [ ] The preview is legible at 128×128 with the default background.
- [ ] The author field credits the creator.
- [ ] The CC0-1.0 checkbox is present in the issue or PR description.

`crates/xhair-app/src/autostart.rs`:

```rs
//! Start-with-OS integration: XDG autostart on Linux, `Run` registry on Windows.

use std::io;
use std::path::PathBuf;

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub const APP_ID: &str = "x-hair";

fn executable() -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
    std::env::current_exe()
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn autostart_file() -> PathBuf {
    let base = std::env::var_os("XDG_CONFIG_HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .or_else(|| std::env::var_os("HOME").map(|home| PathBuf::from(home).join(".config")))
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(".config"));
    base.join("autostart").join(format!("{APP_ID}.desktop"))
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn quote_exec(path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
    let escaped = path
        .to_string_lossy()
        .replace('%', "%%")
        .replace('\\', "\\\\")
        .replace('"', "\\\"");
    format!("\"{escaped}\"")
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub fn set_enabled(enabled: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
    let path = autostart_file();
    if !enabled {
        return match std::fs::remove_file(&path) {
            Ok(()) => Ok(()),
            Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(()),
            Err(error) => Err(error.to_string()),
        };
    }
    let exe = executable().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    let content = format!(
        "[Desktop Entry]\nType=Application\nName=X-Hair\nComment=Crosshair overlay\nExec={}\nTerminal=false\nX-GNOME-Autostart-enabled=true\nCategories=Utility;\n",
        quote_exec(&exe)
    );
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    }
    let temporary = path.with_extension("desktop.tmp");
    std::fs::write(&temporary, content).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    std::fs::rename(&temporary, &path).map_err(|error| error.to_string())
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub fn is_enabled() -> bool {
    autostart_file().is_file()
}

#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
const RUN_KEY: &str = r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run";
#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
const VALUE_NAME: &str = "X-Hair";

#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
pub fn set_enabled(enabled: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
    use windows::core::PCWSTR;
    use windows::Win32::System::Registry::{
        RegCloseKey, RegDeleteValueW, RegOpenKeyExW, RegSetValueExW, HKEY, HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
        KEY_SET_VALUE, REG_SZ,
    };

    fn wide(value: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
        value.encode_utf16().chain(std::iter::once(0)).collect()
    }

    let key_path = wide(RUN_KEY);
    unsafe {
        let mut key = HKEY::default();
        let status = RegOpenKeyExW(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            PCWSTR(key_path.as_ptr()),
            Some(0),
            KEY_SET_VALUE,
            &mut key,
        );
        if status.is_err() {
            return Err(format!("could not open the Run registry key: {status:?}"));
        }
        let result = if enabled {
            let exe = executable().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
            let data = wide(&format!("\"{}\"", exe.display()));
            let name = wide(VALUE_NAME);
            let bytes = std::slice::from_raw_parts(
                data.as_ptr() as *const u8,
                data.len() * std::mem::size_of::<u16>(),
            );
            RegSetValueExW(key, PCWSTR(name.as_ptr()), Some(0), REG_SZ, Some(bytes))
        } else {
            let name = wide(VALUE_NAME);
            RegDeleteValueW(key, PCWSTR(name.as_ptr()))
        };
        let _ = RegCloseKey(key);
        result
            .ok()
            .map_err(|error| format!("could not update the Run registry value: {error}"))
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
pub fn is_enabled() -> bool {
    use windows::core::PCWSTR;
    use windows::Win32::System::Registry::{
        RegCloseKey, RegOpenKeyExW, RegQueryValueExW, HKEY, HKEY_CURRENT_USER, KEY_QUERY_VALUE,
        REG_VALUE_TYPE,
    };

    let key_path: Vec<u16> = RUN_KEY.encode_utf16().chain(std::iter::once(0)).collect();
    let name: Vec<u16> = VALUE_NAME
        .encode_utf16()
        .chain(std::iter::once(0))
        .collect();
    unsafe {
        let mut key = HKEY::default();
        if RegOpenKeyExW(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            PCWSTR(key_path.as_ptr()),
            Some(0),
            KEY_QUERY_VALUE,
            &mut key,
        )
        .is_err()
        {
            return false;
        }
        let mut kind = REG_VALUE_TYPE::default();
        let status = RegQueryValueExW(
            key,
            PCWSTR(name.as_ptr()),
            None,
            Some(&mut kind),
            None,
            None,
        );
        let _ = RegCloseKey(key);
        status.is_ok()
    }
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "windows")))]
pub fn set_enabled(_enabled: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
    Err("start with OS is not supported on this platform".to_string())
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "windows")))]
pub fn is_enabled() -> bool {
    false
}

```

`crates/xhair-app/src/daemon.rs`:

```rs
//! Background daemon: tray, passive input, weapon-slot FSM and overlay.

use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime};

use crossbeam_channel::{unbounded, Receiver, RecvTimeoutError, Sender};
use xhair_core::{
    profile_for_process, AdsBehavior, CrosshairSpec, FireBehavior, KeyCode, Modifier, Trigger,
    XHairConfig,
};
use xhair_platform::{
    create_input_listener, create_overlay, create_window_watcher, InputEvent, OverlayBackend,
    OverlayFrame, WindowEvent,
};
use xhair_render::{render_with, to_bgra_premultiplied, RenderContext};

use crate::{autostart, library};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum TrayCommand {
    Toggle,
    SetProfile(String),
    SetPreset(String),
    SetKeepAbove(bool),
    SetStartWithOs(bool),
    ToggleKeepAbove,
    ToggleStartWithOs,
    OpenStudio,
    SyncCommunity,
    Quit,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Event {
    Input(InputEvent),
    Window(WindowEvent),
    Tray(TrayCommand),
}

/// Snapshot used by the tray loop to keep check marks and the tooltip in sync.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct TrayState {
    pub visible: bool,
    pub keep_above: bool,
    pub start_with_os: bool,
    pub running: bool,
    pub tooltip: String,
    pub active_profile: String,
}

struct State {
    config: XHairConfig,
    config_path: PathBuf,
    last_config_mtime: Option<SystemTime>,
    library: BTreeMap<String, CrosshairSpec>,
    library_errors: Vec<String>,
    current_id: String,
    slot_hidden: bool,
    ads_hidden: bool,
    ads_restore: Option<String>,
    matched_profile: Option<String>,
    pressed: BTreeSet<String>,
    modifiers: Modifiers,
    fire_active: bool,
    bloom: f32,
    bloom_target: f32,
    flash_until: Option<Instant>,
    last_frame: Option<(u32, u32, i32, i32, Vec<u8>)>,
    last_tray: Option<TrayState>,
    running: bool,
    permission_hint_shown: bool,
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct Modifiers {
    alt: bool,
    shift: bool,
    control: bool,
    super_key: bool,
}

impl Modifiers {
    fn set(&mut self, name: &str, down: bool) -> bool {
        match name {
            "Alt" => self.alt = down,
            "Shift" => self.shift = down,
            "Control" => self.control = down,
            "Super" => self.super_key = down,
            _ => return false,
        }
        true
    }

    fn has(&self, modifier: Modifier) -> bool {
        match modifier {
            Modifier::Alt => self.alt,
            Modifier::Shift => self.shift,
            Modifier::Ctrl => self.control,
            Modifier::Super => self.super_key,
        }
    }
}

enum Action {
    Toggle,
    ShowCrosshair(String),
    HideSlot,
    AdsDown,
    AdsUp,
    FireDown,
    FireUp,
}

impl State {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let config_path = xhair_core::config_path();
        let config = match XHairConfig::load_from(&config_path) {
            Ok(config) => config,
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("x-hair: config could not be loaded, using defaults: {error}");
                XHairConfig::default()
            }
        };
        let last_config_mtime = fs_modified(&config_path);
        let (library, library_errors) = library::load();
        for error in &library_errors {
            eprintln!("x-hair: skipping crosshair: {error}");
        }
        let mut state = Self {
            config,
            config_path,
            last_config_mtime,
            library,
            library_errors,
            current_id: String::new(),
            slot_hidden: false,
            ads_hidden: false,
            ads_restore: None,
            matched_profile: None,
            pressed: BTreeSet::new(),
            modifiers: Modifiers::default(),
            fire_active: false,
            bloom: 0.0,
            bloom_target: 0.0,
            flash_until: None,
            last_frame: None,
            last_tray: None,
            running: true,
            permission_hint_shown: false,
        };
        state.reconcile_crosshair();
        state
    }

    fn profile_name(&self) -> String {
        if let Some(matched) = &self.matched_profile {
            if self.config.profiles.iter().any(|p| &p.name == matched) {
                return matched.clone();
            }
        }
        if self
            .config
            .profiles
            .iter()
            .any(|p| p.name == self.config.active_profile)
        {
            self.config.active_profile.clone()
        } else {
            self.config
                .profiles
                .first()
                .map(|p| p.name.clone())
                .unwrap_or_default()
        }
    }

    fn profile(&self) -> xhair_core::GameProfile {
        let name = self.profile_name();
        self.config
            .profiles
            .iter()
            .find(|profile| profile.name == name)
            .cloned()
            .or_else(|| self.config.profiles.first().cloned())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    fn reconcile_crosshair(&mut self) {
        if !self.library.contains_key(&self.current_id) {
            let fallback = self
                .library
                .contains_key(&self.config.last_crosshair)
                .then(|| self.config.last_crosshair.clone())
                .or_else(|| self.library.keys().next().cloned())
                .unwrap_or_default();
            self.current_id = fallback;
        }
        if !self.library.contains_key(&self.config.last_crosshair) {
            self.config.last_crosshair = self.current_id.clone();
        }
    }

    fn set_crosshair(&mut self, id: String) {
        if self.library.contains_key(&id) {
            self.current_id = id;
            self.slot_hidden = false;
            self.config.last_crosshair = self.current_id.clone();
            self.config.push_recent(&self.current_id);
            self.save_config();
        }
    }

    fn save_config(&mut self) {
        match self.config.save_to(&self.config_path) {
            Ok(()) => self.last_config_mtime = fs_modified(&self.config_path),
            Err(error) => eprintln!("x-hair: could not save config: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn handle_input(&mut self, event: InputEvent) {
        let profile = self.profile();
        let mut actions = Vec::new();

        match event {
            InputEvent::KeyDown(key) => {
                if self.modifiers.set(key.as_str(), true) {
                    return;
                }
                if !self.pressed.insert(key.0.clone()) {
                    return;
                }
                if self.hotkey_matches(&profile, &key) {
                    actions.push(Action::Toggle);
                }
                if let Some(binding) = profile.binding_for(&Trigger::Key(key.clone())) {
                    actions.push(slot_action(binding));
                }
            }
            InputEvent::KeyUp(key) => {
                self.modifiers.set(key.as_str(), false);
                self.pressed.remove(&key.0);
            }
            InputEvent::MouseDown(button) => {
                let mouse = &profile.mouse_trigger;
                if button == mouse.ads_button {
                    actions.push(Action::AdsDown);
                }
                if button == mouse.fire_button {
                    actions.push(Action::FireDown);
                }
                if let Some(binding) = profile.binding_for(&Trigger::Mouse(button)) {
                    actions.push(slot_action(binding));
                }
            }
            InputEvent::MouseUp(button) => {
                let mouse = &profile.mouse_trigger;
                if button == mouse.ads_button {
                    actions.push(Action::AdsUp);
                }
                if button == mouse.fire_button {
                    actions.push(Action::FireUp);
                }
            }
            InputEvent::Wheel(delta) => {
                let trigger = if delta >= 0 {
                    Trigger::WheelUp
                } else {
                    Trigger::WheelDown
                };
                if let Some(binding) = profile.binding_for(&trigger) {
                    actions.push(slot_action(binding));
                }
            }
        }

        for action in actions {
            match action {
                Action::Toggle => self.config.overlay_visible = !self.config.overlay_visible,
                Action::ShowCrosshair(id) => self.set_crosshair(id),
                Action::HideSlot => self.slot_hidden = true,
                Action::AdsDown => match profile.ads_behavior.clone() {
                    AdsBehavior::HideOnHold => self.ads_hidden = true,
                    AdsBehavior::SwitchCrosshair(id) => {
                        if self.library.contains_key(&id) {
                            self.ads_restore = Some(self.current_id.clone());
                            self.current_id = id;
                        }
                    }
                    AdsBehavior::None => {}
                },
                Action::AdsUp => {
                    self.ads_hidden = false;
                    if let Some(restore) = self.ads_restore.take() {
                        if self.library.contains_key(&restore) {
                            self.current_id = restore;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Action::FireDown => {
                    self.fire_active = true;
                    match &profile.fire_behavior {
                        FireBehavior::Bloom => self.bloom_target = 1.0,
                        FireBehavior::FlashColor(_) => {
                            self.flash_until = Some(Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(70));
                        }
                        FireBehavior::HideTopArm | FireBehavior::None => {}
                    }
                }
                Action::FireUp => {
                    self.fire_active = false;
                    self.bloom_target = 0.0;
                }
            }
        }

        if matches!(self.profile().fire_behavior, FireBehavior::Bloom) && self.fire_active {
            self.bloom_target = 1.0;
        }
    }

    fn hotkey_matches(&self, profile: &xhair_core::GameProfile, key: &KeyCode) -> bool {
        let hotkey = &profile.master_toggle_hotkey;
        hotkey.key == *key
            && hotkey
                .modifiers
                .iter()
                .all(|modifier| self.modifiers.has(*modifier))
            && hotkey.modifiers.len() == self.active_modifier_count()
    }

    fn active_modifier_count(&self) -> usize {
        [
            self.modifiers.alt,
            self.modifiers.shift,
            self.modifiers.control,
            self.modifiers.super_key,
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|active| *active)
        .count()
    }

    fn handle_window(&mut self, event: WindowEvent) {
        let previous = self.profile_name();
        self.matched_profile = event
            .process
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(|process| profile_for_process(&self.config.profiles, process))
            .map(|profile| profile.name.clone());
        if self.profile_name() != previous {
            let profile = self.profile();
            if self.library.contains_key(&profile.default_crosshair_id) {
                self.current_id = profile.default_crosshair_id.clone();
            }
            self.slot_hidden = false;
            self.ads_hidden = false;
            self.ads_restore = None;
        }
    }

    fn handle_tray(&mut self, command: TrayCommand, overlay: &mut Option<Box<dyn OverlayBackend>>) {
        let command = match command {
            TrayCommand::ToggleKeepAbove => {
                TrayCommand::SetKeepAbove(!self.config.keep_above_fullscreen)
            }
            TrayCommand::ToggleStartWithOs => {
                TrayCommand::SetStartWithOs(!self.config.start_with_os)
            }
            other => other,
        };
        match command {
            TrayCommand::Toggle => {
                self.config.overlay_visible = !self.config.overlay_visible;
                self.save_config();
            }
            TrayCommand::SetProfile(name) => {
                if self
                    .config
                    .profiles
                    .iter()
                    .any(|profile| profile.name == name)
                {
                    self.config.active_profile = name;
                    self.matched_profile = None;
                    let profile = self.profile();
                    if self.library.contains_key(&profile.default_crosshair_id) {
                        self.current_id = profile.default_crosshair_id.clone();
                    }
                    self.slot_hidden = false;
                    self.save_config();
                }
            }
            TrayCommand::SetPreset(id) => self.set_crosshair(id),
            TrayCommand::SetKeepAbove(enabled) => {
                self.config.keep_above_fullscreen = enabled;
                if let Some(overlay) = overlay.as_mut() {
                    if let Err(error) = overlay.set_keep_above(enabled) {
                        eprintln!("x-hair: could not update z-order: {error}");
                    }
                }
                self.save_config();
            }
            TrayCommand::SetStartWithOs(enabled) => match autostart::set_enabled(enabled) {
                Ok(()) => {
                    self.config.start_with_os = enabled;
                    self.save_config();
                }
                Err(error) => eprintln!("x-hair: could not update autostart: {error}"),
            },
            TrayCommand::OpenStudio => spawn_studio(false),
            TrayCommand::SyncCommunity => {
                std::thread::spawn(|| match xhair_community::sync_default() {
                    Ok(outcome) => eprintln!(
                        "x-hair: community catalog refreshed ({} entries{})",
                        outcome.catalog.entries.len(),
                        if outcome.from_cache {
                            ", offline cache"
                        } else {
                            ""
                        }
                    ),
                    Err(error) => eprintln!("x-hair: community sync failed: {error}"),
                });
            }
            TrayCommand::Quit => self.running = false,
            TrayCommand::ToggleKeepAbove | TrayCommand::ToggleStartWithOs => {}
        }
    }

    fn maybe_reload(&mut self) {
        let Some(mtime) = fs_modified(&self.config_path) else {
            return;
        };
        if Some(mtime) == self.last_config_mtime {
            return;
        }
        match XHairConfig::load_from(&self.config_path) {
            Ok(config) => {
                self.config = config;
                self.last_config_mtime = Some(mtime);
                let (library, errors) = library::load();
                self.library = library;
                self.library_errors = errors;
                for error in &self.library_errors {
                    eprintln!("x-hair: skipping crosshair: {error}");
                }
                self.reconcile_crosshair();
            }
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("x-hair: ignoring invalid config update: {error}");
                self.last_config_mtime = Some(mtime);
            }
        }
    }

    fn tick(&mut self) {
        let bloom_enabled = matches!(self.profile().fire_behavior, FireBehavior::Bloom);
        if bloom_enabled {
            let delta = self.bloom_target - self.bloom;
            if delta.abs() > 0.002 {
                self.bloom += delta * 0.35;
            } else if self.bloom != self.bloom_target {
                self.bloom = self.bloom_target;
            }
        } else {
            self.bloom = 0.0;
        }
        if self
            .flash_until
            .is_some_and(|until| Instant::now() >= until)
        {
            self.flash_until = None;
        }
    }

    fn paint(&mut self, overlay: &mut Option<Box<dyn OverlayBackend>>) {
        let profile = self.profile();
        let visible = self.config.overlay_visible && !self.slot_hidden && !self.ads_hidden;
        if !visible {
            if let Some(overlay) = overlay.as_mut() {
                if overlay.is_visible() {
                    if let Err(error) = overlay.hide() {
                        eprintln!("x-hair: overlay hide failed: {error}");
                    }
                }
            }
            self.last_frame = None;
            self.publish_tray("X-Hair — hidden");
            return;
        }

        let Some(spec) = self.library.get(&self.current_id).cloned() else {
            return;
        };
        let ctx = RenderContext {
            on_fire: self.fire_active && matches!(profile.fire_behavior, FireBehavior::HideTopArm),
            bloom: self.bloom.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            flash: match profile.fire_behavior {
                FireBehavior::FlashColor(color) if self.flash_until.is_some() => Some(color),
                _ => None,
            },
        };
        let pixmap = render_with(&spec, ctx);
        let width = pixmap.width();
        let height = pixmap.height();
        let bgra = to_bgra_premultiplied(&pixmap);
        let signature = (width, height, spec.offset_x, spec.offset_y);
        if let Some((last_w, last_h, last_x, last_y, last_bytes)) = &self.last_frame {
            if (*last_w, *last_h, *last_x, *last_y) == signature && last_bytes == &bgra {
                return;
            }
        }

        let frame = OverlayFrame::new(width, height, spec.offset_x, spec.offset_y, bgra.clone());
        if let Some(overlay) = overlay.as_mut() {
            let result = if overlay.is_visible() {
                overlay.update(&frame)
            } else {
                overlay.show(&frame)
            };
            if let Err(error) = result {
                eprintln!("x-hair: overlay paint failed: {error}");
            }
        }
        self.last_frame = Some((signature.0, signature.1, signature.2, signature.3, bgra));
        self.publish_tray(&format!("X-Hair — {}", spec.name));
    }

    fn publish_tray(&mut self, tooltip: &str) {
        let state = TrayState {
            visible: self.config.overlay_visible,
            keep_above: self.config.keep_above_fullscreen,
            start_with_os: self.config.start_with_os,
            running: self.running,
            tooltip: tooltip.to_string(),
            active_profile: self.profile_name(),
        };
        if self.last_tray.as_ref().is_some_and(|last| {
            last.visible == state.visible
                && last.keep_above == state.keep_above
                && last.start_with_os == state.start_with_os
                && last.running == state.running
                && last.tooltip == state.tooltip
                && last.active_profile == state.active_profile
        }) {
            return;
        }
        self.last_tray = Some(state);
    }
}

fn slot_action(binding: &xhair_core::SlotBinding) -> Action {
    match &binding.crosshair_id {
        Some(id) if binding.mode == xhair_core::BindingMode::Switch => {
            Action::ShowCrosshair(id.clone())
        }
        _ => Action::HideSlot,
    }
}

fn fs_modified(path: &std::path::Path) -> Option<SystemTime> {
    std::fs::metadata(path).ok()?.modified().ok()
}

fn forward_input(rx: Receiver<InputEvent>, tx: Sender<Event>) {
    for event in rx.iter() {
        if tx.send(Event::Input(event)).is_err() {
            break;
        }
    }
}

fn forward_window(rx: Receiver<WindowEvent>, tx: Sender<Event>) {
    for event in rx.iter() {
        if tx.send(Event::Window(event)).is_err() {
            break;
        }
    }
}

/// Start the daemon with a caller-provided event receiver.
pub fn start_with_receiver(
    event_rx: Receiver<Event>,
    tray_tx: Sender<TrayState>,
    quit: Arc<AtomicBool>,
) -> std::thread::JoinHandle<()> {
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("xhair-core".to_string())
        .spawn(move || run_core(event_rx, tray_tx, quit))
        .expect("spawn xhair core thread")
}

fn run_core(event_rx: Receiver<Event>, tray_tx: Sender<TrayState>, quit: Arc<AtomicBool>) {
    let mut state = State::new();
    if state.config.start_with_os && !autostart::is_enabled() {
        if let Err(error) = autostart::set_enabled(true) {
            eprintln!("x-hair: could not re-enable autostart: {error}");
        }
    }
    let (merged_tx, merged_rx) = unbounded::<Event>();

    let merge_event = {
        let merged = merged_tx.clone();
        std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("xhair-event-merge".to_string())
            .spawn(move || {
                for event in event_rx.iter() {
                    if merged.send(event).is_err() {
                        break;
                    }
                }
            })
            .ok()
    };

    let (input_tx, input_rx) = unbounded();
    let mut input = create_input_listener();
    match input.start(input_tx) {
        Ok(()) => {}
        Err(error) => eprintln!("x-hair: global input unavailable: {error}"),
    }
    if let Some(hint) = input.permission_hint() {
        if state.config.notifications && !state.permission_hint_shown {
            eprintln!("x-hair: {hint}");
            state.permission_hint_shown = true;
        }
    }
    let input_forwarder = std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("xhair-input-fwd".to_string())
        .spawn({
            let tx = merged_tx.clone();
            move || forward_input(input_rx, tx)
        })
        .ok();

    let (window_tx, window_rx) = unbounded();
    let mut watcher = create_window_watcher();
    if let Err(error) = watcher.start(window_tx) {
        eprintln!("x-hair: active-window detection unavailable: {error}");
    }
    let window_forwarder = std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("xhair-window-fwd".to_string())
        .spawn({
            let tx = merged_tx.clone();
            move || forward_window(window_rx, tx)
        })
        .ok();

    let mut overlay = match create_overlay() {
        Ok(overlay) => Some(overlay),
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("x-hair: overlay unavailable: {error}");
            None
        }
    };
    if let Some(overlay) = overlay.as_mut() {
        if let Err(error) = overlay.set_keep_above(state.config.keep_above_fullscreen) {
            eprintln!("x-hair: could not set z-order: {error}");
        }
    }

    state.paint(&mut overlay);
    if let Some(snapshot) = state.last_tray.clone() {
        let _ = tray_tx.try_send(snapshot);
    }

    while state.running && !quit.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
        match merged_rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(80)) {
            Ok(Event::Input(event)) => {
                state.handle_input(event);
                state.paint(&mut overlay);
                if let Some(snapshot) = state.last_tray.clone() {
                    let _ = tray_tx.try_send(snapshot);
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Window(event)) => {
                state.handle_window(event);
                state.paint(&mut overlay);
                if let Some(snapshot) = state.last_tray.clone() {
                    let _ = tray_tx.try_send(snapshot);
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Tray(command)) => {
                state.handle_tray(command, &mut overlay);
                state.paint(&mut overlay);
                if let Some(snapshot) = state.last_tray.clone() {
                    let _ = tray_tx.try_send(snapshot);
                }
            }
            Err(RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => {
                state.maybe_reload();
                state.tick();
                state.paint(&mut overlay);
                if let Some(snapshot) = state.last_tray.clone() {
                    let _ = tray_tx.try_send(snapshot);
                }
            }
            Err(RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => break,
        }
    }

    if let Some(overlay) = overlay.as_mut() {
        let _ = overlay.hide();
    }
    state.save_config();
    quit.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
    input.stop();
    watcher.stop();
    drop(input_forwarder);
    drop(merge_event);
    drop(window_forwarder);
}

/// Launch the Studio as a separate process so all of its GPU/font memory is
/// reclaimed when the window closes.
pub fn spawn_studio(open_community: bool) {
    let Ok(exe) = std::env::current_exe() else {
        eprintln!("x-hair: could not locate the executable to open the Studio");
        return;
    };
    let mut command = std::process::Command::new(exe);
    command.arg("--studio");
    if open_community {
        command.arg("--community");
    }
    match command.spawn() {
        Ok(_) => {}
        Err(error) => eprintln!("x-hair: could not open the Studio: {error}"),
    }
}

`crates/xhair-app/src/icon.rs`:

```rs
//! Procedural X-Hair tray mark (no third-party image assets).

use xhair_core::{CrosshairCategory, CrosshairSpec, DotShape, GameTag, ReticleLayer, Rgba};
use xhair_render::{render, to_straight_rgba};

const SIZE: u32 = 32;

pub fn tray_icon() -> tray_icon::Icon {
    let spec = CrosshairSpec::new(
        "x-hair-icon",
        "X-Hair",
        "X-Hair",
        GameTag::Universal,
        CrosshairCategory::Community,
    )
    .with_canvas(SIZE)
    .with_layers(vec![
        ReticleLayer::diagonal_x(9.0, 2.4, 2.2, Rgba::CYAN, 0.0, Rgba::BLACK),
        ReticleLayer::center_dot(1.5, DotShape::Circle, Rgba::WHITE, 0.0, Rgba::BLACK),
    ]);
    let pixmap = render(&spec);
    let rgba = to_straight_rgba(&pixmap);
    tray_icon::Icon::from_rgba(rgba, SIZE, SIZE).unwrap_or_else(|_| {
        let transparent = vec![0u8; (SIZE * SIZE * 4) as usize];
        tray_icon::Icon::from_rgba(transparent, SIZE, SIZE).expect("transparent icon")
    })
}

```

`crates/xhair-app/src/library.rs`:

```rs
//! Built-in plus user crosshair library shared by the daemon and Studio.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use xhair_core::{presets, validate_spec, CrosshairSpec};

/// Directory that stores crosshairs created or imported by the user.
pub fn user_dir() -> PathBuf {
    xhair_core::config_dir().join("crosshairs")
}

/// Every built-in preset keyed by id.
pub fn builtins() -> BTreeMap<String, CrosshairSpec> {
    presets::all_presets()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|spec| (spec.id.clone(), spec))
        .collect()
}

/// Load user crosshairs, skipping (and reporting) invalid files.
pub fn load_user() -> (BTreeMap<String, CrosshairSpec>, Vec<String>) {
    let mut library = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    let directory = user_dir();
    let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(&directory) else {
        return (library, errors);
    };
    let mut paths: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
        .filter_map(Result::ok)
        .map(|entry| entry.path())
        .filter(|path| path.extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == "json"))
        .collect();
    paths.sort();
    for path in paths {
        match read_spec(&path) {
            Ok(spec) => {
                library.insert(spec.id.clone(), spec);
            }
            Err(error) => errors.push(format!("{}: {error}", path.display())),
        }
    }
    (library, errors)
}

fn read_spec(path: &Path) -> Result<CrosshairSpec, String> {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    let spec: CrosshairSpec = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    validate_spec(&spec).map_err(|errors| errors.to_string())?;
    Ok(spec)
}

/// Load built-ins overlaid with user crosshairs plus any load errors.
pub fn load() -> (BTreeMap<String, CrosshairSpec>, Vec<String>) {
    let (user, errors) = load_user();
    let mut library = builtins();
    library.extend(user);
    (library, errors)
}

/// Persist a user crosshair, validating it and its id first.
pub fn save_user(spec: &CrosshairSpec) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    if !is_safe_id(&spec.id) {
        return Err(format!("unsafe crosshair id: {}", spec.id));
    }
    validate_spec(spec).map_err(|errors| errors.to_string())?;
    let directory = user_dir();
    fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    let path = directory.join(format!("{}.json", spec.id));
    let temporary = directory.join(format!(".{}.json.tmp", spec.id));
    let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(spec).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    fs::write(&temporary, json).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    fs::rename(&temporary, &path).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    Ok(path)
}

/// Remove a user crosshair by id. Built-ins are never touched.
pub fn delete_user(id: &str) -> io::Result<bool> {
    if !is_safe_id(id) {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
            "unsafe crosshair id",
        ));
    }
    let path = user_dir().join(format!("{id}.json"));
    match fs::remove_file(path) {
        Ok(()) => Ok(true),
        Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(false),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}

/// Whether `id` can be used as a file name without escaping the user directory.
pub fn is_safe_id(id: &str) -> bool {
    let bytes = id.as_bytes();
    !bytes.is_empty()
        && bytes.len() <= 64
        && !id.starts_with('-')
        && !id.ends_with('-')
        && !id.contains("--")
        && bytes
            .iter()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_lowercase() || byte.is_ascii_digit() || *byte == b'-')
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn ids_cannot_escape_the_user_directory() {
        assert!(is_safe_id("my-crosshair"));
        assert!(!is_safe_id("../escape"));
        assert!(!is_safe_id("a/b"));
        assert!(!is_safe_id("C:evil"));
        assert!(!is_safe_id("UPPER"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_builtin_is_present() {
        assert_eq!(builtins().len(), xhair_core::presets::all_presets().len());
    }
}

`crates/xhair-app/src/main.rs`:

```rs
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

//! X-Hair: tray daemon and on-demand crosshair studio.

mod autostart;
mod daemon;
mod icon;
mod library;
mod studio;
mod tray;

use std::fs::OpenOptions;
use std::process::ExitCode;

use fs2::FileExt;

const USAGE: &str = "\
X-Hair — anti-cheat-safe crosshair overlay

USAGE:
    x-hair              Start the tray daemon
    x-hair --studio     Open the Studio window (spawned on demand by the tray)
    x-hair --help       Show this help
    x-hair --version    Print the version

The daemon needs no elevation. Global weapon-slot switching on Linux requires
membership of the `input` group (see the README).";

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    xhair_platform::configure_process();
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    if args.iter().any(|arg| arg == "--help" || arg == "-h") {
        println!("{USAGE}");
        return ExitCode::SUCCESS;
    }
    if args.iter().any(|arg| arg == "--version" || arg == "-V") {
        println!("x-hair {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
        return ExitCode::SUCCESS;
    }
    if args.iter().any(|arg| arg == "--studio") {
        studio::run(args.iter().any(|arg| arg == "--community"));
        return ExitCode::SUCCESS;
    }

    let _lock = match acquire_lock() {
        Ok(lock) => lock,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("x-hair: {error}");
            return ExitCode::SUCCESS;
        }
    };

    match tray::run() {
        Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("x-hair: {error}");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn acquire_lock() -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    let directory = xhair_core::config_dir();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    let path = directory.join("xhair.lock");
    let file = OpenOptions::new()
        .create(true)
        .read(true)
        .write(true)
        .truncate(false)
        .open(&path)
        .map_err(|error| format!("could not open {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    file.try_lock_exclusive()
        .map_err(|_| "X-Hair is already running".to_string())?;
    Ok(file)
}
