`crates/xhair-app/src/studio.rs`:

```rs
//! On-demand egui Studio: library, designer, profiles and community browser.

use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap};
use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};

use crossbeam_channel::Receiver;
use eframe::egui;
use xhair_community::{Catalog, CatalogEntry};
use xhair_core::{
    presets, share_code, AdsBehavior, BracketStyle, CrosshairCategory, CrosshairSpec, DotShape,
    FireBehavior, GameTag, HotkeyCombo, Modifier, ReticleLayer, Rgba, TShape,
};
use xhair_render::{render, to_straight_rgba};

use crate::library;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Tab {
    Library,
    Designer,
    Profiles,
    Community,
}

enum UiAction {
    Apply(String),
    Favorite(String),
    Design(String),
    DeleteUser(String),
    AddLayer(ReticleLayer),
    RemoveLayer(usize),
    MoveLayer(usize, isize),
    SaveDraft,
    DeleteDraft,
}

pub fn run(open_community: bool) {
    let options = eframe::NativeOptions {
        viewport: egui::ViewportBuilder::default()
            .with_inner_size([1060.0, 740.0])
            .with_min_inner_size([780.0, 540.0])
            .with_title("X-Hair Studio"),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let result = eframe::run_native(
        "X-Hair Studio",
        options,
        Box::new(move |cc| Ok(Box::new(Studio::new(cc, open_community)))),
    );
    if let Err(error) = result {
        eprintln!("x-hair: studio could not start: {error}");
    }
}

struct Studio {
    config: xhair_core::XHairConfig,
    library: BTreeMap<String, CrosshairSpec>,
    library_errors: Vec<String>,
    tab: Tab,
    status: String,
    search: String,
    game_filter: Option<GameTag>,
    favorites_only: bool,
    textures: HashMap<String, (u64, egui::TextureHandle)>,
    selected_id: String,
    draft: CrosshairSpec,
    share_input: String,
    share_output: String,
    catalog: Option<Catalog>,
    catalog_message: String,
    catalog_rx: Option<Receiver<Result<Catalog, String>>>,
    catalog_search: String,
    catalog_game: Option<GameTag>,
    catalog_specs: HashMap<String, Option<CrosshairSpec>>,
}

impl Studio {
    fn new(cc: &eframe::CreationContext<'_>, open_community: bool) -> Self {
        let config = xhair_core::XHairConfig::load().unwrap_or_default();
        let (library, library_errors) = library::load();
        let selected_id = if library.contains_key(&config.last_crosshair) {
            config.last_crosshair.clone()
        } else {
            library.keys().next().cloned().unwrap_or_default()
        };
        let draft = library
            .get(&selected_id)
            .cloned()
            .or_else(|| presets::all_presets().into_iter().next())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| {
                CrosshairSpec::new(
                    "my-crosshair",
                    "My Crosshair",
                    "You",
                    GameTag::Universal,
                    CrosshairCategory::Community,
                )
            });
        let (catalog, catalog_message) = match xhair_community::load_cached() {
            Ok(catalog) => {
                let message = format!("{} cached crosshair(s)", catalog.entries.len());
                (Some(catalog), message)
            }
            Err(_) => (None, "No catalog yet — press Sync now".to_string()),
        };
        cc.egui_ctx.set_visuals(egui::Visuals::dark());
        Self {
            config,
            library,
            library_errors,
            tab: if open_community {
                Tab::Community
            } else {
                Tab::Library
            },
            status: String::new(),
            search: String::new(),
            game_filter: None,
            favorites_only: false,
            textures: HashMap::new(),
            selected_id,
            draft,
            share_input: String::new(),
            share_output: String::new(),
            catalog,
            catalog_message,
            catalog_rx: None,
            catalog_search: String::new(),
            catalog_game: None,
            catalog_specs: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }

    fn persist(&mut self) {
        match self.config.save() {
            Ok(()) => self.status = "Configuration saved".to_string(),
            Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not save config: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn apply(&mut self, id: &str) {
        if !self.library.contains_key(id) {
            self.status = format!("Unknown crosshair {id}");
            return;
        }
        self.config.last_crosshair = id.to_string();
        self.config.push_recent(id);
        self.selected_id = id.to_string();
        self.status = format!("Applied {}", self.library[id].name);
        self.persist();
    }

    fn toggle_favorite(&mut self, id: &str) {
        let favorite = self.config.toggle_favorite(id);
        self.status = if favorite {
            "Added to favorites".to_string()
        } else {
            "Removed from favorites".to_string()
        };
        self.persist();
    }

    fn load_into_designer(&mut self, id: &str) {
        if let Some(spec) = self.library.get(id).cloned() {
            self.draft = spec;
            self.selected_id = id.to_string();
            self.share_output.clear();
            self.tab = Tab::Designer;
            self.status = "Loaded into the designer".to_string();
        }
    }

    fn save_draft(&mut self) {
        match library::save_user(&self.draft) {
            Ok(path) => {
                self.library
                    .insert(self.draft.id.clone(), self.draft.clone());
                self.selected_id = self.draft.id.clone();
                self.config.last_crosshair = self.draft.id.clone();
                self.config.push_recent(&self.draft.id);
                self.textures.remove("draft");
                self.status = format!("Saved {}", path.display());
                self.persist();
            }
            Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not save crosshair: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn delete_user_crosshair(&mut self, id: &str) {
        match library::delete_user(id) {
            Ok(true) => {
                self.library.remove(id);
                self.textures.remove(&format!("lib:{id}"));
                if self.config.last_crosshair == id {
                    if let Some(next) = self.library.keys().next().cloned() {
                        self.config.last_crosshair = next;
                    }
                }
                self.status = format!("Deleted {id}");
                self.persist();
            }
            Ok(false) => self.status = "This crosshair is not a user file".to_string(),
            Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not delete: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn delete_draft(&mut self) {
        let id = self.draft.id.clone();
        match library::delete_user(&id) {
            Ok(true) => {
                let (library, errors) = library::load();
                self.library = library;
                self.library_errors = errors;
                if self.library.contains_key(&id) {
                    self.status = "Built-in presets cannot be deleted".to_string();
                } else {
                    self.status = format!("Deleted {id}");
                }
                self.textures.remove("draft");
            }
            Ok(false) => self.status = "This crosshair is not a user file".to_string(),
            Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not delete: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn import_share_code(&mut self) {
        let code = self.share_input.trim();
        if code.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        match xhair_core::decode(code) {
            Ok(spec) => match xhair_core::validate_spec(&spec) {
                Ok(()) => {
                    self.draft = spec;
                    self.selected_id = self.draft.id.clone();
                    self.share_output = code.to_string();
                    self.status = "Share code imported".to_string();
                }
                Err(errors) => self.status = format!("Invalid crosshair: {errors}"),
            },
            Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not decode share code: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn export_share_code(&mut self) {
        match share_code::encode(&self.draft) {
            Ok(code) => {
                self.share_output = code;
                self.status = "Share code generated".to_string();
            }
            Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not encode: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn start_sync(&mut self) {
        if self.catalog_rx.is_some() {
            return;
        }
        let (tx, rx) = crossbeam_channel::unbounded();
        self.catalog_rx = Some(rx);
        self.catalog_message = "Syncing…".to_string();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let result = xhair_community::sync_default()
                .map(|outcome| outcome.catalog)
                .map_err(|error| error.to_string());
            let _ = tx.send(result);
        });
    }

    fn poll_sync(&mut self) {
        let Some(rx) = self.catalog_rx.as_ref() else {
            return;
        };
        match rx.try_recv() {
            Ok(Ok(catalog)) => {
                self.catalog_message = format!("{} crosshair(s)", catalog.entries.len());
                self.catalog_specs.clear();
                self.catalog = Some(catalog);
                self.catalog_rx = None;
            }
            Ok(Err(error)) => {
                self.catalog_message = format!("Sync failed: {error}");
                self.catalog_rx = None;
            }
            Err(crossbeam_channel::TryRecvError::Empty) => {}
            Err(crossbeam_channel::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {
                self.catalog_message = "Sync worker stopped".to_string();
                self.catalog_rx = None;
            }
        }
    }

    fn catalog_spec(&mut self, entry: &CatalogEntry) -> Option<CrosshairSpec> {
        if let Some(cached) = self.catalog_specs.get(&entry.id) {
            return cached.clone();
        }
        let decoded = entry.to_spec().ok();
        self.catalog_specs.insert(entry.id.clone(), decoded.clone());
        decoded
    }

    fn import_catalog(&mut self, id: &str) {
        let Some(entry) = self
            .catalog
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|catalog| catalog.by_id(id))
            .cloned()
        else {
            self.status = "Catalog entry disappeared".to_string();
            return;
        };
        match entry.to_spec() {
            Ok(spec) => match library::save_user(&spec) {
                Ok(_) => {
                    self.library.insert(spec.id.clone(), spec.clone());
                    self.apply(&spec.id);
                    self.status = format!("Imported {}", spec.name);
                }
                Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not import: {error}"),
            },
            Err(error) => self.status = format!("Rejected catalog entry: {error}"),
        }
    }

    fn texture_for(
        &mut self,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        key: &str,
        spec: &CrosshairSpec,
    ) -> egui::TextureHandle {
        let signature = spec_signature(spec);
        if let Some((cached, texture)) = self.textures.get(key) {
            if *cached == signature {
                return texture.clone();
            }
        }
        let texture = make_texture(ctx, key, spec);
        self.textures
            .insert(key.to_string(), (signature, texture.clone()));
        texture
    }
}

fn spec_signature(spec: &CrosshairSpec) -> u64 {
    let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
    serde_json::to_vec(spec)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .hash(&mut hasher);
    hasher.finish()
}

fn make_texture(ctx: &egui::Context, key: &str, spec: &CrosshairSpec) -> egui::TextureHandle {
    let pixmap = render(spec);
    let size = [pixmap.width() as usize, pixmap.height() as usize];
    let rgba = to_straight_rgba(&pixmap);
    let image = egui::ColorImage::from_rgba_unmultiplied(size, &rgba);
    ctx.load_texture(key.to_string(), image, egui::TextureOptions::LINEAR)
}

fn color_edit(ui: &mut egui::Ui, label: &str, color: &mut Rgba) {
    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
        ui.label(label);
        let mut value = egui::Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(color.r, color.g, color.b, color.a);
        if ui.color_edit_button_srgba(&mut value).changed() {
            *color = Rgba::new(value.r(), value.g(), value.b(), value.a());
        }
    });
}

fn drag(ui: &mut egui::Ui, label: &str, value: &mut f32, range: std::ops::RangeInclusive<f32>) {
    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
        ui.label(label);
        ui.add(egui::DragValue::new(value).speed(0.1).range(range));
    });
}

impl eframe::App for Studio {
    fn ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, _frame: &mut eframe::Frame) {
        let ctx = ui.ctx().clone();
        self.poll_sync();

        egui::Panel::top("tabs").show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.heading("X-Hair Studio");
                ui.separator();
                ui.selectable_value(&mut self.tab, Tab::Library, "Library");
                ui.selectable_value(&mut self.tab, Tab::Designer, "Designer");
                ui.selectable_value(&mut self.tab, Tab::Profiles, "Profiles");
                ui.selectable_value(&mut self.tab, Tab::Community, "Community");
            });
        });

        egui::Panel::bottom("status").show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label(if self.status.is_empty() {
                    "Ready"
                } else {
                    &self.status
                });
                if !self.library_errors.is_empty() {
                    ui.separator();
                    ui.colored_label(
                        egui::Color32::YELLOW,
                        format!("{} crosshair file(s) skipped", self.library_errors.len()),
                    );
                }
            });
        });

        egui::CentralPanel::default().show(ui, |ui| match self.tab {
            Tab::Library => self.library_ui(ui, &ctx),
            Tab::Designer => self.designer_ui(ui, &ctx),
            Tab::Profiles => self.profiles_ui(ui),
            Tab::Community => self.community_ui(ui, &ctx),
        });
    }
}

impl Studio {
    fn library_ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.label("Search");
            ui.text_edit_singleline(&mut self.search);
            ui.label("Game");
            egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt("library-game")
                .selected_text(self.game_filter.map(GameTag::label).unwrap_or("All games"))
                .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                    ui.selectable_value(&mut self.game_filter, None, "All games");
                    for game in GameTag::ALL {
                        ui.selectable_value(&mut self.game_filter, Some(game), game.label());
                    }
                });
            ui.checkbox(&mut self.favorites_only, "Favorites");
        });

        let needle = self.search.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
        let ids: Vec<String> = self
            .library
            .iter()
            .filter(|(id, spec)| {
                (self.game_filter.is_none() || Some(spec.game) == self.game_filter)
                    && (!self.favorites_only || self.config.is_favorite(id))
                    && (needle.is_empty()
                        || spec.name.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle)
                        || id.contains(&needle)
                        || spec
                            .tags
                            .iter()
                            .any(|tag| tag.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle)))
            })
            .map(|(id, _)| id.clone())
            .collect();

        ui.separator();
        let mut action: Option<UiAction> = None;
        egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
            .auto_shrink([false; 2])
            .show(ui, |ui| {
                for chunk in ids.chunks(4) {
                    ui.horizontal_top(|ui| {
                        for id in chunk {
                            let Some(spec) = self.library.get(id).cloned() else {
                                continue;
                            };
                            let texture = self.texture_for(ctx, &format!("lib:{id}"), &spec);
                            ui.allocate_ui(egui::vec2(236.0, 236.0), |ui| {
                                egui::Frame::group(ui.style()).show(ui, |ui| {
                                    ui.set_min_size(egui::vec2(220.0, 220.0));
                                    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                                        ui.add(
                                            egui::Image::from_texture(
                                                egui::load::SizedTexture::new(
                                                    texture.id(),
                                                    egui::vec2(96.0, 96.0),
                                                ),
                                            )
                                            .fit_to_exact_size(egui::vec2(96.0, 96.0)),
                                        );
                                        ui.strong(&spec.name);
                                        ui.small(format!(
                                            "{} · {}",
                                            spec.game.label(),
                                            spec.category.label()
                                        ));
                                        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                            if ui.button("Apply").clicked() {
                                                action = Some(UiAction::Apply(id.clone()));
                                            }
                                            if ui
                                                .button(if self.config.is_favorite(id) {
                                                    "★"
                                                } else {
                                                    "☆"
                                                })
                                                .clicked()
                                            {
                                                action = Some(UiAction::Favorite(id.clone()));
                                            }
                                            if ui.button("Edit").clicked() {
                                                action = Some(UiAction::Design(id.clone()));
                                            }
                                            let user_file = library::user_dir()
                                                .join(format!("{id}.json"))
                                                .exists();
                                            if user_file && ui.button("Delete").clicked() {
                                                action = Some(UiAction::DeleteUser(id.clone()));
                                            }
                                        });
                                    });
                                });
                            });
                        }
                    });
                }
            });

        if let Some(action) = action {
            match action {
                UiAction::Apply(id) => self.apply(&id),
                UiAction::Favorite(id) => self.toggle_favorite(&id),
                UiAction::Design(id) => self.load_into_designer(&id),
                UiAction::DeleteUser(id) => self.delete_user_crosshair(&id),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }

    fn designer_ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        let draft = self.draft.clone();
        let texture = self.texture_for(ctx, "draft", &draft);
        let mut actions: Vec<UiAction> = Vec::new();

        ui.columns(2, |columns| {
            columns[0].vertical_centered(|ui| {
                ui.add(
                    egui::Image::from_texture(egui::load::SizedTexture::new(
                        texture.id(),
                        egui::vec2(256.0, 256.0),
                    ))
                    .fit_to_exact_size(egui::vec2(256.0, 256.0)),
                );
                ui.label(format!(
                    "{} · {} · {} px",
                    self.draft.game.label(),
                    self.draft.category.label(),
                    self.draft.canvas_size
                ));
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    if ui.button("Apply").clicked() {
                        let id = self.draft.id.clone();
                        actions.push(UiAction::Apply(id));
                    }
                    if ui.button("Save to library").clicked() {
                        actions.push(UiAction::SaveDraft);
                    }
                    if ui.button("Delete user copy").clicked() {
                        actions.push(UiAction::DeleteDraft);
                    }
                });
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                ui.label("Import / export");
                ui.add(egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.share_input).hint_text("XHAIR1:…"));
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    if ui.button("Import code").clicked() {
                        self.import_share_code();
                    }
                    if ui.button("Generate code").clicked() {
                        self.export_share_code();
                    }
                });
                if !self.share_output.is_empty() {
                    ui.add(
                        egui::TextEdit::multiline(&mut self.share_output)
                            .desired_rows(4)
                            .code_editor(),
                    );
                    if ui.button("Copy code").clicked() {
                        ui.ctx().copy_text(self.share_output.clone());
                    }
                }
            });

            egui::ScrollArea::vertical().show(&mut columns[1], |ui| {
                ui.label("Name");
                ui.text_edit_singleline(&mut self.draft.name);
                ui.label("Author");
                ui.text_edit_singleline(&mut self.draft.author);
                ui.label("ID (kebab-case)");
                ui.text_edit_singleline(&mut self.draft.id);
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label("Game");
                    egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt("draft-game")
                        .selected_text(self.draft.game.label())
                        .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                            for game in GameTag::ALL {
                                ui.selectable_value(&mut self.draft.game, game, game.label());
                            }
                        });
                });
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label("Category");
                    egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt("draft-category")
                        .selected_text(self.draft.category.label())
                        .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                            for category in CrosshairCategory::ALL {
                                ui.selectable_value(
                                    &mut self.draft.category,
                                    category,
                                    category.label(),
                                );
                            }
                        });
                });
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label("Canvas");
                    ui.add(
                        egui::DragValue::new(&mut self.draft.canvas_size)
                            .speed(1)
                            .range(16..=256),
                    );
                    ui.label("X offset");
                    ui.add(egui::DragValue::new(&mut self.draft.offset_x).range(-150..=150));
                    ui.label("Y offset");
                    ui.add(egui::DragValue::new(&mut self.draft.offset_y).range(-150..=150));
                });
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label("Opacity");
                    ui.add(
                        egui::Slider::new(&mut self.draft.global_opacity, 0.0..=1.0)
                            .fixed_decimals(2),
                    );
                });

                ui.separator();
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    let mut bloom = self.draft.bloom.is_some();
                    if ui.checkbox(&mut bloom, "Bloom on fire").changed() {
                        self.draft.bloom = bloom.then(xhair_core::BloomConfig::default);
                    }
                    if let Some(bloom) = self.draft.bloom.as_mut() {
                        ui.add(
                            egui::DragValue::new(&mut bloom.trigger_ms)
                                .prefix("delay ")
                                .suffix(" ms")
                                .range(0..=1000),
                        );
                        ui.add(
                            egui::DragValue::new(&mut bloom.expand_px)
                                .prefix("spread ")
                                .suffix(" px")
                                .range(0.0..=32.0),
                        );
                        ui.add(
                            egui::Slider::new(&mut bloom.max_scale, 1.0..=3.0)
                                .prefix("scale ")
                                .fixed_decimals(2),
                        );
                        ui.add(
                            egui::DragValue::new(&mut bloom.fade_ms)
                                .prefix("fade ")
                                .suffix(" ms")
                                .range(0..=1000),
                        );
                    }
                });

                ui.separator();
                ui.strong("Layers");
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    if ui.button("+ Lines").clicked() {
                        actions.push(UiAction::AddLayer(ReticleLayer::lines(
                            6.0,
                            1.5,
                            3.0,
                            0.0,
                            Rgba::WHITE,
                            0.0,
                            Rgba::BLACK,
                            TShape::Never,
                            false,
                        )));
                    }
                    if ui.button("+ Dot").clicked() {
                        actions.push(UiAction::AddLayer(ReticleLayer::center_dot(
                            1.5,
                            DotShape::Circle,
                            Rgba::WHITE,
                            0.0,
                            Rgba::BLACK,
                        )));
                    }
                    if ui.button("+ Ring").clicked() {
                        actions.push(UiAction::AddLayer(ReticleLayer::circle_ring(
                            10.0,
                            1.5,
                            Rgba::WHITE,
                            0.0,
                            Rgba::BLACK,
                            4,
                            12.0,
                            0.0,
                        )));
                    }
                });

                let mut remove: Option<usize> = None;
                let mut shift: Option<(usize, isize)> = None;
                for index in 0..self.draft.layers.len() {
                    let mut layer = self.draft.layers[index].clone();
                    let title = layer_title(&layer);
                    ui.push_id(index, |ui| {
                        egui::CollapsingHeader::new(format!("{index}. {title}"))
                            .default_open(index == 0)
                            .show(ui, |ui| {
                                layer_edit(ui, &mut layer);
                                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                    if ui.button("Up").clicked() {
                                        shift = Some((index, -1));
                                    }
                                    if ui.button("Down").clicked() {
                                        shift = Some((index, 1));
                                    }
                                    if ui.button("Remove").clicked() {
                                        remove = Some(index);
                                    }
                                });
                            });
                    });
                    self.draft.layers[index] = layer;
                }
                self.process_layer_actions(&mut actions, remove, shift);
            });
        });

        for action in actions {
            match action {
                UiAction::Apply(id) => self.apply(&id),
                UiAction::SaveDraft => self.save_draft(),
                UiAction::DeleteDraft => self.delete_draft(),
                UiAction::AddLayer(layer) => {
                    if self.draft.layers.len() < 8 {
                        self.draft.layers.push(layer);
                    } else {
                        self.status = "A crosshair can have at most 8 layers".to_string();
                    }
                }
                UiAction::RemoveLayer(index) => {
                    if index < self.draft.layers.len() {
                        self.draft.layers.remove(index);
                    }
                }
                UiAction::MoveLayer(index, delta) => {
                    let target = index as isize + delta;
                    if target >= 0 && target < self.draft.layers.len() as isize {
                        self.draft.layers.swap(index, target as usize);
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }

    fn process_layer_actions(
        &mut self,
        actions: &mut Vec<UiAction>,
        remove: Option<usize>,
        shift: Option<(usize, isize)>,
    ) {
        if let Some(index) = remove {
            actions.push(UiAction::RemoveLayer(index));
        }
        if let Some((index, delta)) = shift {
            actions.push(UiAction::MoveLayer(index, delta));
        }
    }

    fn profiles_ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let names: Vec<String> = self
            .config
            .profiles
            .iter()
            .map(|p| p.name.clone())
            .collect();
        let mut selected = self.config.active_profile.clone();
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.label("Active profile");
            egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt("active-profile")
                .selected_text(selected.clone())
                .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                    for name in &names {
                        ui.selectable_value(&mut selected, name.clone(), name);
                    }
                });
        });
        if selected != self.config.active_profile {
            self.config.active_profile = selected;
            self.persist();
        }

        let ids: Vec<String> = self.library.keys().cloned().collect();
        let mut changed = false;
        let active = self.config.active_profile.clone();
        let Some(profile) = self
            .config
            .profiles
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|profile| profile.name == active)
        else {
            ui.label("No profile selected");
            return;
        };

        egui::ScrollArea::vertical().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.heading(&profile.name);
            ui.label("Process names (comma separated)");
            let mut processes = profile.process_names.join(", ");
            if ui.text_edit_singleline(&mut processes).changed() {
                profile.process_names = processes
                    .split(',')
                    .map(str::trim)
                    .filter(|name| !name.is_empty())
                    .map(str::to_string)
                    .collect();
                changed = true;
            }

            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("Default crosshair");
                egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt("profile-default")
                    .selected_text(
                        self.library
                            .get(&profile.default_crosshair_id)
                            .map(|spec| spec.name.clone())
                            .unwrap_or_else(|| profile.default_crosshair_id.clone()),
                    )
                    .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                        for id in &ids {
                            if ui
                                .selectable_value(&mut profile.default_crosshair_id, id.clone(), id)
                                .changed()
                            {
                                changed = true;
                            }
                        }
                    });
            });

            ui.separator();
            ui.label("ADS behaviour (right mouse by default)");
            let mut ads = profile.ads_behavior.clone();
            let mut ads_changed = false;
            ui.radio_value(&mut ads, AdsBehavior::None, "Unchanged");
            ui.radio_value(&mut ads, AdsBehavior::HideOnHold, "Hide while held");
            ui.radio_value(
                &mut ads,
                AdsBehavior::SwitchCrosshair(profile.default_crosshair_id.clone()),
                "Switch crosshair",
            );
            if let AdsBehavior::SwitchCrosshair(id) = &mut ads {
                egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt("ads-crosshair")
                    .selected_text(id.clone())
                    .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                        for candidate in &ids {
                            if ui
                                .selectable_value(id, candidate.clone(), candidate)
                                .changed()
                            {
                                ads_changed = true;
                            }
                        }
                    });
            }
            if ads != profile.ads_behavior || ads_changed {
                profile.ads_behavior = ads;
                changed = true;
            }

            ui.separator();
            ui.label("Fire behaviour");
            let mut fire = profile.fire_behavior.clone();
            let mut fire_changed = false;
            ui.radio_value(&mut fire, FireBehavior::None, "None");
            ui.radio_value(&mut fire, FireBehavior::Bloom, "Bloom");
            ui.radio_value(&mut fire, FireBehavior::HideTopArm, "Hide top arm");
            let flash_color = match &fire {
                FireBehavior::FlashColor(color) => *color,
                _ => Rgba::RED,
            };
            let mut color = flash_color;
            color_edit(ui, "Flash color", &mut color);
            if color != flash_color {
                fire = FireBehavior::FlashColor(color);
                fire_changed = true;
            }
            if fire != profile.fire_behavior || fire_changed {
                profile.fire_behavior = fire;
                changed = true;
            }

            ui.separator();
            ui.label("Master toggle hotkey");
            let mut hotkey = profile.master_toggle_hotkey.clone();
            let mut hotkey_changed = false;
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                for (label, modifier) in [
                    ("Alt", Modifier::Alt),
                    ("Shift", Modifier::Shift),
                    ("Ctrl", Modifier::Ctrl),
                    ("Super", Modifier::Super),
                ] {
                    let mut active = hotkey.modifiers.contains(&modifier);
                    if ui.checkbox(&mut active, label).changed() {
                        if active {
                            hotkey.modifiers.push(modifier);
                        } else {
                            hotkey.modifiers.retain(|existing| *existing != modifier);
                        }
                        hotkey_changed = true;
                    }
                }
                ui.label("Key");
                let mut key = hotkey.key.to_string();
                if ui.text_edit_singleline(&mut key).changed() {
                    hotkey.key = xhair_core::KeyCode(key);
                    hotkey_changed = true;
                }
            });
            if hotkey_changed {
                profile.master_toggle_hotkey = HotkeyCombo {
                    modifiers: hotkey.modifiers,
                    key: hotkey.key,
                };
                changed = true;
            }

            ui.separator();
            ui.label("Weapon slots");
            let profile_name = profile.name.clone();
            for binding in &mut profile.slot_bindings {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label(format!("{:>10}", binding.trigger.label()));
                    let current = binding
                        .crosshair_id
                        .clone()
                        .unwrap_or_else(|| "(hidden)".to_string());
                    egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt(format!(
                        "slot-{}-{}",
                        profile_name,
                        binding.trigger.label()
                    ))
                    .selected_text(current)
                    .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                        if ui
                            .selectable_value(&mut binding.crosshair_id, None, "(hidden)")
                            .changed()
                        {
                            binding.mode = xhair_core::BindingMode::Hide;
                            changed = true;
                        }
                        for id in &ids {
                            if ui
                                .selectable_value(&mut binding.crosshair_id, Some(id.clone()), id)
                                .changed()
                            {
                                binding.mode = xhair_core::BindingMode::Switch;
                                changed = true;
                            }
                        }
                    });
                });
            }
        });

        if changed {
            self.persist();
        }
    }

    fn community_ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            if ui
                .add_enabled(self.catalog_rx.is_none(), egui::Button::new("Sync now"))
                .clicked()
            {
                self.start_sync();
            }
            ui.label(&self.catalog_message);
            if ui.button("Submission page").clicked() {
                match xhair_community::submission_url(&self.draft) {
                    Ok(url) => {
                        open_url(&url);
                        self.status = "Opened GitHub submission page".to_string();
                    }
                    Err(error) => self.status = format!("Could not build URL: {error}"),
                }
            }
        });
        ui.separator();

        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.label("Search");
            ui.text_edit_singleline(&mut self.catalog_search);
            ui.label("Game");
            egui::ComboBox::from_id_salt("catalog-game")
                .selected_text(self.catalog_game.map(GameTag::label).unwrap_or("All games"))
                .show_ui(ui, |ui| {
                    ui.selectable_value(&mut self.catalog_game, None, "All games");
                    for game in GameTag::ALL {
                        ui.selectable_value(&mut self.catalog_game, Some(game), game.label());
                    }
                });
        });

        let Some(catalog) = self.catalog.clone() else {
            ui.add_space(24.0);
            ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                ui.label("The community catalog is empty.");
                ui.label("Sync it from GitHub Pages to browse contributed crosshairs.");
            });
            return;
        };

        let needle = self.catalog_search.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
        let entries: Vec<CatalogEntry> = catalog
            .entries
            .iter()
            .filter(|entry| self.catalog_game.is_none() || Some(entry.game) == self.catalog_game)
            .filter(|entry| {
                needle.is_empty()
                    || entry.name.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle)
                    || entry.author.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle)
                    || entry.id.contains(&needle)
            })
            .cloned()
            .collect();

        let mut import: Option<String> = None;
        egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
            .auto_shrink([false; 2])
            .show(ui, |ui| {
                for entry in &entries {
                    let spec = self.catalog_spec(entry);
                    ui.group(|ui| {
                        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                            if let Some(spec) = &spec {
                                let texture =
                                    self.texture_for(ctx, &format!("community:{}", entry.id), spec);
                                ui.add(
                                    egui::Image::from_texture(egui::load::SizedTexture::new(
                                        texture.id(),
                                        egui::vec2(64.0, 64.0),
                                    ))
                                    .fit_to_exact_size(egui::vec2(64.0, 64.0)),
                                );
                            } else {
                                ui.allocate_space(egui::vec2(64.0, 64.0));
                            }
                            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                                ui.strong(&entry.name);
                                ui.label(format!(
                                    "{} · {} · by {}",
                                    entry.game.label(),
                                    entry.category.label(),
                                    entry.author
                                ));
                                if spec.is_none() {
                                    ui.colored_label(
                                        egui::Color32::RED,
                                        "Invalid or tampered entry",
                                    );
                                }
                                if ui.button("Import").clicked() {
                                    import = Some(entry.id.clone());
                                }
                            });
                        });
                    });
                }
            });

        if let Some(id) = import {
            self.import_catalog(&id);
        }
    }
}

fn layer_title(layer: &ReticleLayer) -> &'static str {
    match layer {
        ReticleLayer::Lines { .. } => "Lines",
        ReticleLayer::CenterDot { .. } => "Center dot",
        ReticleLayer::CircleRing { .. } => "Circle ring",
        ReticleLayer::Brackets { .. } => "Brackets",
        ReticleLayer::DiagonalX { .. } => "Diagonal X",
        ReticleLayer::CustomImage { .. } => "Custom image",
    }
}

fn layer_edit(ui: &mut egui::Ui, layer: &mut ReticleLayer) {
    match layer {
        ReticleLayer::Lines {
            length,
            thickness,
            gap,
            rotation_deg,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
            t_shape,
            rounded_caps,
        } => {
            drag(ui, "Length", length, 0.0..=64.0);
            drag(ui, "Thickness", thickness, 0.1..=16.0);
            drag(ui, "Gap", gap, 0.0..=64.0);
            drag(ui, "Rotation", rotation_deg, -180.0..=180.0);
            drag(ui, "Outline", outline_thickness, 0.0..=8.0);
            color_edit(ui, "Color", color);
            color_edit(ui, "Outline color", outline_color);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("T-shape");
                for (value, label) in [
                    (TShape::Never, "Never"),
                    (TShape::Always, "Always"),
                    (TShape::OnFire, "On fire"),
                ] {
                    ui.selectable_value(t_shape, value, label);
                }
            });
            ui.checkbox(rounded_caps, "Rounded caps");
        }
        ReticleLayer::CenterDot {
            radius,
            shape,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
        } => {
            drag(ui, "Radius", radius, 0.1..=24.0);
            drag(ui, "Outline", outline_thickness, 0.0..=8.0);
            color_edit(ui, "Color", color);
            color_edit(ui, "Outline color", outline_color);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("Shape");
                for (value, label) in [
                    (DotShape::Circle, "Circle"),
                    (DotShape::Square, "Square"),
                    (DotShape::Diamond, "Diamond"),
                ] {
                    ui.selectable_value(shape, value, label);
                }
            });
        }
        ReticleLayer::CircleRing {
            radius,
            thickness,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
            segments,
            arc_gap_deg,
            rotation_deg,
        } => {
            drag(ui, "Radius", radius, 1.0..=100.0);
            drag(ui, "Thickness", thickness, 0.1..=16.0);
            drag(ui, "Outline", outline_thickness, 0.0..=8.0);
            drag(ui, "Gap", arc_gap_deg, 0.0..=180.0);
            drag(ui, "Rotation", rotation_deg, -180.0..=180.0);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("Segments");
                ui.add(egui::DragValue::new(segments).range(1..=32));
            });
            color_edit(ui, "Color", color);
            color_edit(ui, "Outline color", outline_color);
        }
        ReticleLayer::Brackets {
            distance,
            arm_length,
            thickness,
            style,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
        } => {
            drag(ui, "Distance", distance, 1.0..=100.0);
            drag(ui, "Arm length", arm_length, 1.0..=64.0);
            drag(ui, "Thickness", thickness, 0.1..=16.0);
            drag(ui, "Outline", outline_thickness, 0.0..=8.0);
            color_edit(ui, "Color", color);
            color_edit(ui, "Outline color", outline_color);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("Style");
                for (value, label) in [
                    (BracketStyle::SquareCorner, "Square"),
                    (BracketStyle::AngledChevron, "Chevron"),
                    (BracketStyle::RangefinderTicks, "Ticks"),
                ] {
                    ui.selectable_value(style, value, label);
                }
            });
        }
        ReticleLayer::DiagonalX {
            length,
            thickness,
            gap,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
        } => {
            drag(ui, "Length", length, 0.0..=64.0);
            drag(ui, "Thickness", thickness, 0.1..=16.0);
            drag(ui, "Gap", gap, 0.0..=64.0);
            drag(ui, "Outline", outline_thickness, 0.0..=8.0);
            color_edit(ui, "Color", color);
            color_edit(ui, "Outline color", outline_color);
        }
        ReticleLayer::CustomImage {
            base64_png_or_svg,
            scale,
            tint_color,
        } => {
            ui.label(format!(
                "Embedded image ({} base64 bytes)",
                base64_png_or_svg.len()
            ));
            drag(ui, "Scale", scale, 0.05..=8.0);
            let mut tinted = tint_color.is_some();
            if ui.checkbox(&mut tinted, "Tint").changed() {
                *tint_color = tinted.then_some(Rgba::WHITE);
            }
            if let Some(color) = tint_color {
                color_edit(ui, "Tint color", color);
            }
        }
    }
}

fn open_url(url: &str) {
    if !url.starts_with("https://github.com/") {
        return;
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    let result = std::process::Command::new("xdg-open").arg(url).spawn();
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    let result = std::process::Command::new("explorer").arg(url).spawn();
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "windows")))]
    let result: Result<std::process::Child, std::io::Error> =
        Err(std::io::Error::other("unsupported platform"));
    if let Err(error) = result {
        eprintln!("x-hair: could not open the browser: {error}");
    }
}
```

`crates/xhair-app/src/tray.rs`:

```rs
//! System tray construction and the platform event loop that keeps it alive.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use crossbeam_channel::{unbounded, Receiver, Sender};
use muda::{CheckMenuItem, Menu, MenuEvent, MenuItem, PredefinedMenuItem, Submenu};
use tray_icon::{TrayIcon, TrayIconBuilder};
use xhair_core::XHairConfig;

use crate::daemon::{self, Event, TrayCommand, TrayState};
use crate::{icon, library};

pub const TOGGLE_ID: &str = "xhair.toggle";
pub const STUDIO_ID: &str = "xhair.studio";
pub const SYNC_ID: &str = "xhair.sync";
pub const KEEP_ID: &str = "xhair.keep";
pub const START_ID: &str = "xhair.start";
pub const QUIT_ID: &str = "xhair.quit";
pub const PROFILE_PREFIX: &str = "xhair.profile:";
pub const PRESET_PREFIX: &str = "xhair.preset:";

struct Handles {
    toggle: CheckMenuItem,
    keep: CheckMenuItem,
    start: CheckMenuItem,
    profiles: Vec<(String, CheckMenuItem)>,
}

fn parse(id: &str) -> Option<TrayCommand> {
    match id {
        TOGGLE_ID => Some(TrayCommand::Toggle),
        STUDIO_ID => Some(TrayCommand::OpenStudio),
        SYNC_ID => Some(TrayCommand::SyncCommunity),

        QUIT_ID => Some(TrayCommand::Quit),
        other if other.starts_with(PROFILE_PREFIX) => Some(TrayCommand::SetProfile(
            other[PROFILE_PREFIX.len()..].to_string(),
        )),
        other if other.starts_with(PRESET_PREFIX) => Some(TrayCommand::SetPreset(
            other[PRESET_PREFIX.len()..].to_string(),
        )),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn build_menu(config: &XHairConfig) -> Result<(Menu, Handles), String> {
    let menu = Menu::new();

    let toggle = CheckMenuItem::with_id(
        TOGGLE_ID,
        "Toggle Overlay",
        true,
        config.overlay_visible,
        None,
    );
    let studio = MenuItem::with_id(STUDIO_ID, "Open X-Hair Studio…", true, None);
    let sync = MenuItem::with_id(SYNC_ID, "Sync Community Hub", true, None);

    let mut profile_items = Vec::new();
    let profiles = Submenu::new("Active Profile", true);
    for profile in &config.profiles {
        let id = format!("{PROFILE_PREFIX}{}", profile.name);
        let item = CheckMenuItem::with_id(
            id,
            &profile.name,
            true,
            profile.name == config.active_profile,
            None,
        );
        profiles.append(&item).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        profile_items.push((profile.name.clone(), item));
    }

    let mut preset_items: Vec<String> = config.favorites.clone();
    preset_items.extend(config.recent.iter().cloned());
    preset_items.dedup();
    let presets = Submenu::new("Quick Presets", true);
    let (catalog, _) = library::load();
    let mut preset_count = 0;
    for id in &preset_items {
        let Some(spec) = catalog.get(id) else {
            continue;
        };
        let item = MenuItem::with_id(
            format!("{PRESET_PREFIX}{}", spec.id),
            &spec.name,
            true,
            None,
        );
        presets.append(&item).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        preset_count += 1;
    }
    if preset_count == 0 {
        let placeholder = MenuItem::with_id(
            "xhair.preset.placeholder",
            "Favorite a crosshair to see it here",
            false,
            None,
        );
        presets
            .append(&placeholder)
            .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    }

    let keep = CheckMenuItem::with_id(
        KEEP_ID,
        "Keep Above Fullscreen",
        true,
        config.keep_above_fullscreen,
        None,
    );
    let start = CheckMenuItem::with_id(START_ID, "Start With OS", true, config.start_with_os, None);
    let quit = MenuItem::with_id(QUIT_ID, "Quit X-Hair", true, None);

    let separator = PredefinedMenuItem::separator();
    let separator2 = PredefinedMenuItem::separator();
    let separator3 = PredefinedMenuItem::separator();

    menu.append_items(&[
        &toggle,
        &studio,
        &separator,
        &profiles,
        &presets,
        &separator2,
        &keep,
        &start,
        &sync,
        &separator3,
        &quit,
    ])
    .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;

    Ok((
        menu,
        Handles {
            toggle,
            keep,
            start,
            profiles: profile_items,
        },
    ))
}

fn build_tray(config: &XHairConfig) -> Result<(TrayIcon, Handles), String> {
    let (menu, handles) = build_menu(config)?;
    let tray = TrayIconBuilder::new()
        .with_menu(Box::new(menu))
        .with_tooltip("X-Hair — crosshair overlay")
        .with_icon(icon::tray_icon())
        .build()
        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
    Ok((tray, handles))
}

fn install_menu_handler(tx: Sender<Event>) {
    MenuEvent::set_event_handler(Some(move |event: MenuEvent| {
        let id = event.id().0.clone();
        // Check menu items only report "clicked"; the daemon owns the actual
        // state, so toggles must invert the current value rather than trust
        // the menu item's local check flag.
        let command = match id.as_str() {
            KEEP_ID => Some(TrayCommand::ToggleKeepAbove),
            START_ID => Some(TrayCommand::ToggleStartWithOs),
            _ => parse(&id),
        };
        if let Some(command) = command {
            let _ = tx.send(Event::Tray(command));
        }
    }));
}

fn apply_state(handles: &Handles, tray: &TrayIcon, state: &TrayState) {
    handles.toggle.set_checked(state.visible);
    handles.keep.set_checked(state.keep_above);
    handles.start.set_checked(state.start_with_os);
    for (name, item) in &handles.profiles {
        item.set_checked(name == &state.active_profile);
    }
    let _ = tray.set_tooltip(Some(&state.tooltip));
}

/// Run the tray on the main thread and the daemon core on a worker thread.
pub fn run() -> Result<(), String> {
    let (event_tx, event_rx) = unbounded();
    let (tray_tx, tray_rx) = unbounded();
    let quit = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));

    let core = daemon::start_with_receiver(event_rx, tray_tx, Arc::clone(&quit));
    let result = platform_loop(event_tx, tray_rx, quit);
    let _ = core.join();
    result
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn platform_loop(
    event_tx: Sender<Event>,
    tray_rx: Receiver<TrayState>,
    quit: Arc<AtomicBool>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    gtk::init().map_err(|error| format!("GTK could not start: {error}"))?;
    install_menu_handler(event_tx);
    let config = XHairConfig::load().unwrap_or_default();
    let (tray, handles) = build_tray(&config)?;

    let mut last: Option<TrayState> = None;
    gtk::glib::timeout_add_local(Duration::from_millis(100), move || {
        while let Ok(state) = tray_rx.try_recv() {
            last = Some(state);
        }
        if let Some(state) = &last {
            apply_state(&handles, &tray, state);
            if !state.running || quit.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                gtk::main_quit();
                return gtk::glib::ControlFlow::Break;
            }
        }
        gtk::glib::ControlFlow::Continue
    });
    gtk::main();
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
fn platform_loop(
    event_tx: Sender<Event>,
    tray_rx: Receiver<TrayState>,
    quit: Arc<AtomicBool>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    install_menu_handler(event_tx);
    let config = XHairConfig::load().unwrap_or_default();
    let (tray, handles) = build_tray(&config)?;
    let mut last: Option<TrayState> = None;
    loop {
        pump_messages();
        while let Ok(state) = tray_rx.try_recv() {
            last = Some(state);
        }
        if let Some(state) = &last {
            apply_state(&handles, &tray, state);
            if !state.running || quit.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                break;
            }
        }
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(40));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
fn pump_messages() {
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
        DispatchMessageW, PeekMessageW, TranslateMessage, MSG, PM_REMOVE,
    };
    unsafe {
        let mut message: MSG = std::mem::zeroed();
        while PeekMessageW(&mut message, None, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE).as_bool() {
            let _ = TranslateMessage(&message);
            DispatchMessageW(&message);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "windows")))]
fn platform_loop(
    _event_tx: Sender<Event>,
    _tray_rx: Receiver<TrayState>,
    _quit: Arc<AtomicBool>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    Err("the tray is only implemented on Linux and Windows".to_string())
}
```

`crates/xhair-cli/src/main.rs`:

```rs
//! Headless tooling for X-Hair: validation, rendering, share codes and the
//! community index build used by the GitHub Pages workflow.

use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::ExitCode;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use xhair_core::{
    presets, share_code, validate_json, validate_spec, CrosshairSpec, GameTag, BUILTIN_SCHEMA,
};
use xhair_render::{export_png, export_svg};

const USAGE: &str = "\
xhair-cli — headless X-Hair tooling

USAGE:
    xhair-cli <command> [arguments]

COMMANDS:
    list [--game <tag>]                 List built-in crosshairs
    show <id|file>                      Print a crosshair as pretty JSON
    validate [path ...]                 Validate crosshair JSON files
    render-png <id|file> <out.png>      Render a crosshair to PNG
    render-svg <id|file> <out.svg>      Render a crosshair to SVG
    build-index <dir> <out-dir>         Build the community catalog and previews
    encode <id|file>                    Print an XHAIR1 share code
    decode <code|file> <out.json>       Decode a share code to JSON
    submission <id|file>                Print a prefilled GitHub submission URL
    schema                              Print the community JSON Schema
    version                             Print the version

A crosshair argument may be a built-in id (e.g. valorant-tenz), a JSON file,
or an XHAIR1 share code.";

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    match run(&args) {
        Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("error: {error}");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn run(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let Some(command) = args.first().map(String::as_str) else {
        println!("{USAGE}");
        return Ok(());
    };
    let rest = &args[1..];
    match command {
        "help" | "-h" | "--help" => {
            println!("{USAGE}");
            Ok(())
        }
        "version" | "-V" | "--version" => {
            println!("x-hair {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
            Ok(())
        }
        "schema" => {
            println!("{BUILTIN_SCHEMA}");
            Ok(())
        }
        "list" => cmd_list(rest),
        "show" => {
            let spec = load_spec(rest.first().ok_or("usage: show <id|file>")?)?;
            println!(
                "{}",
                serde_json::to_string_pretty(&spec).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
            );
            Ok(())
        }
        "validate" => cmd_validate(rest),
        "render-png" => {
            let (source, out) = two_args(rest, "render-png <id|file> <out.png>")?;
            let spec = load_spec(source)?;
            export_png(&spec, out).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            println!("wrote {out}");
            Ok(())
        }
        "render-svg" => {
            let (source, out) = two_args(rest, "render-svg <id|file> <out.svg>")?;
            let spec = load_spec(source)?;
            export_svg(&spec, out).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            println!("wrote {out}");
            Ok(())
        }
        "build-index" => {
            let (dir, out) = two_args(rest, "build-index <dir> <out-dir>")?;
            cmd_build_index(Path::new(dir), Path::new(out))
        }
        "encode" => {
            let spec = load_spec(rest.first().ok_or("usage: encode <id|file>")?)?;
            println!("{}", share_code::encode(&spec).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?);
            Ok(())
        }
        "decode" => {
            let (source, out) = two_args(rest, "decode <code|file> <out.json>")?;
            let spec = load_share_code(source)?;
            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&spec).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(parent) = Path::new(out).parent() {
                if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
                    fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            fs::write(out, json).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            println!("wrote {out}");
            Ok(())
        }
        "submission" => {
            let spec = load_spec(rest.first().ok_or("usage: submission <id|file>")?)?;
            println!(
                "{}",
                xhair_community::submission_url(&spec).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
            );
            Ok(())
        }
        other => Err(format!("unknown command {other:?}\n\n{USAGE}")),
    }
}

fn two_args<'a>(args: &'a [String], usage: &str) -> Result<(&'a str, &'a str), String> {
    match args {
        [first, second, ..] => Ok((first, second)),
        _ => Err(format!("usage: {usage}")),
    }
}

fn cmd_list(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut filter: Option<GameTag> = None;
    let mut index = 0;
    while index < args.len() {
        match args[index].as_str() {
            "--game" => {
                let value = args.get(index + 1).ok_or("--game needs a value")?;
                filter = Some(parse_game(value)?);
                index += 2;
            }
            other => return Err(format!("unknown list option {other:?}")),
        }
    }
    let mut all = presets::all_presets();
    all.sort_by(|a, b| {
        a.game
            .slug()
            .cmp(b.game.slug())
            .then_with(|| a.name.cmp(&b.name))
    });
    let mut current = "";
    for spec in all {
        if let Some(filter) = filter {
            if spec.game != filter {
                continue;
            }
        }
        if spec.game.slug() != current {
            current = spec.game.slug();
            println!("\n{}", spec.game.label());
        }
        println!("  {:<32} {}", spec.id, spec.name);
    }
    println!();
    Ok(())
}

fn parse_game(value: &str) -> Result<GameTag, String> {
    GameTag::ALL
        .into_iter()
        .find(|game| {
            game.slug().eq_ignore_ascii_case(value) || game.label().eq_ignore_ascii_case(value)
        })
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("unknown game {value:?}"))
}

fn cmd_validate(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    if args.is_empty() {
        let default = Path::new("community/crosshairs");
        if default.is_dir() {
            collect_json(default, &mut files)?;
        } else {
            let mut failures = 0usize;
            for spec in presets::all_presets() {
                if let Err(errors) = validate_spec(&spec) {
                    eprintln!("{}: {}", spec.id, errors);
                    failures += 1;
                }
            }
            if failures > 0 {
                return Err(format!("{failures} built-in preset(s) failed validation"));
            }
            println!(
                "{} built-in presets are valid",
                presets::all_presets().len()
            );
            return Ok(());
        }
    } else {
        for arg in args {
            let path = Path::new(arg);
            if path.is_dir() {
                collect_json(path, &mut files)?;
            } else {
                files.push(path.to_path_buf());
            }
        }
    }

    let mut failures = 0usize;
    for file in &files {
        match validate_file(file) {
            Ok(spec) => println!("ok    {} ({})", file.display(), spec.id),
            Err(error) => {
                failures += 1;
                eprintln!("FAIL  {}\n{error}", file.display());
            }
        }
    }
    println!("\n{} file(s) checked, {failures} failure(s)", files.len());
    if failures == 0 {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(format!("{failures} file(s) failed validation"))
    }
}

fn validate_file(path: &Path) -> Result<CrosshairSpec, String> {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    validate_json(BUILTIN_SCHEMA, &value).map_err(|errors| errors.to_string())?;
    serde_json::from_value(value).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

fn collect_json(dir: &Path, out: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let entries = fs::read_dir(dir).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", dir.display()))?;
    let mut children: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
        .filter_map(Result::ok)
        .map(|entry| entry.path())
        .collect();
    children.sort();
    for child in children {
        if child.is_dir() {
            collect_json(&child, out)?;
        } else if child.extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == "json") {
            out.push(child);
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn cmd_build_index(dir: &Path, out: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    collect_json(dir, &mut files)?;
    if files.is_empty() {
        return Err(format!(
            "no crosshair JSON files found in {}",
            dir.display()
        ));
    }

    let mut entries = Vec::with_capacity(files.len());
    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    for file in &files {
        let spec = validate_file(file)?;
        if !seen.insert(spec.id.clone()) {
            return Err(format!("duplicate crosshair id: {}", spec.id));
        }
        let preview = format!("previews/{}/{}.png", spec.game.slug(), spec.id);
        let preview_path = out.join(&preview);
        if let Some(parent) = preview_path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        }
        export_png(&spec, &preview_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let spec_path = out
            .join("crosshairs")
            .join(spec.game.slug())
            .join(format!("{}.json", spec.id));
        if let Some(parent) = spec_path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        }
        fs::write(
            &spec_path,
            serde_json::to_string_pretty(&spec).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        entries.push(
            xhair_community::CatalogEntry::from_spec(&spec, Some(preview))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
        );
    }
    entries.sort_by(|a, b| {
        a.game
            .slug()
            .cmp(b.game.slug())
            .then_with(|| a.id.cmp(&b.id))
    });
    let catalog = xhair_community::Catalog {
        schema_version: xhair_community::INDEX_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        generated_at: now_rfc3339(),
        entries,
    };
    catalog.validate().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    fs::create_dir_all(out).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    fs::write(
        out.join("index.json"),
        serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&catalog).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    println!(
        "indexed {} crosshair(s) into {}",
        catalog.entries.len(),
        out.join("index.json").display()
    );
    Ok(())
}

fn load_spec(source: &str) -> Result<CrosshairSpec, String> {
    if source.starts_with(share_code::PREFIX) {
        return load_share_code(source);
    }
    let path = Path::new(source);
    if source.ends_with(".json") || path.exists() {
        let text = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| format!("{source}: {e}"))?;
        let value: serde_json::Value =
            serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| format!("{source}: {e}"))?;
        if let Err(errors) = validate_json(BUILTIN_SCHEMA, &value) {
            return Err(format!("{source} failed schema validation:\n{errors}"));
        }
        return serde_json::from_value(value).map_err(|e| format!("{source}: {e}"));
    }
    presets::by_id(source).ok_or_else(|| {
        format!("{source} is not a built-in id, a JSON file or an XHAIR1 share code")
    })
}

fn load_share_code(source: &str) -> Result<CrosshairSpec, String> {
    let code = if source.starts_with(share_code::PREFIX) {
        source.to_string()
    } else {
        fs::read_to_string(source)
            .map_err(|e| format!("{source}: {e}"))?
            .trim()
            .to_string()
    };
    let spec = share_code::decode(&code).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    validate_spec(&spec).map_err(|errors| errors.to_string())?;
    Ok(spec)
}

fn now_rfc3339() -> String {
    let seconds = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|duration| duration.as_secs())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let days = (seconds / 86_400) as i64;
    let time = seconds % 86_400;
    let (year, month, day) = civil_from_days(days);
    format!(
        "{year:04}-{month:02}-{day:02}T{:02}:{:02}:{:02}Z",
        time / 3600,
        (time % 3600) / 60,
        time % 60
    )
}

// Howard Hinnant's days-to-civil algorithm.
fn civil_from_days(days: i64) -> (i64, u32, u32) {
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = if z >= 0 { z } else { z - 146_096 } / 146_097;
    let doe = z - era * 146_097;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let y = yoe + era * 400;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let d = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let m = mp + if mp < 10 { 3 } else { -9 };
    (y + i64::from(m <= 2), m as u32, d as u32)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn dates_are_converted() {
        assert_eq!(civil_from_days(0), (1970, 1, 1));
        assert_eq!(civil_from_days(19_000), (2022, 1, 8));
    }
}
```
