`crates/xhair-community/src/lib.rs`:

```rs
//! Community catalog: a GitHub-hosted index of contributed crosshairs.
//!
//! The catalog is a single static `index.json` published through GitHub Pages.
//! Each entry embeds the crosshair as an `XHAIR1:` share code plus a SHA-256
//! digest, so the app can validate a contribution without trusting the index
//! blindly. Syncing is a plain outbound HTTPS `GET` with ETag caching; nothing
//! is ever uploaded by this crate.

use std::fs;
use std::io::{self, Read};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::Duration;

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use thiserror::Error;
use xhair_core::share_code;
use xhair_core::{validate_spec, CrosshairCategory, CrosshairSpec, GameTag};

/// GitHub organization/user that hosts the public catalog.
pub const REPO_OWNER: &str = "ybreakless";
/// Repository name that hosts the public catalog.
pub const REPO_NAME: &str = "x-hair";
/// Default public index URL (GitHub Pages).
pub const DEFAULT_INDEX_URL: &str = "https://ybreakless.github.io/x-hair/index.json";
/// Index document schema version.
pub const INDEX_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 1;
/// Upper bound on an index download. A 100-crosshair catalog is well under it.
pub const MAX_INDEX_BYTES: u64 = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
pub const MAX_CATALOG_ENTRIES: usize = 10_000;

const USER_AGENT: &str = concat!("X-Hair/", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
const INDEX_FILE: &str = "community-index.json";
const ETAG_FILE: &str = "community-index.etag";

/// One catalog record. Metadata is duplicated from the spec so browsing does
/// not require decoding every share code.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CatalogEntry {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub author: String,
    pub game: GameTag,
    pub category: CrosshairCategory,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub tags: Vec<String>,
    /// Relative preview path inside the published catalog, e.g. `previews/valorant/tenz.png`.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub preview: Option<String>,
    /// SHA-256 over the canonical JSON of the spec.
    pub sha256: String,
    /// Portable share code containing the full crosshair.
    pub share_code: String,
}

impl CatalogEntry {
    /// Build an entry from a spec, computing the share code and digest.
    pub fn from_spec(spec: &CrosshairSpec, preview: Option<String>) -> Result<Self, CatalogError> {
        let canonical = canonical_json(spec)?;
        Ok(Self {
            id: spec.id.clone(),
            name: spec.name.clone(),
            author: spec.author.clone(),
            game: spec.game,
            category: spec.category,
            tags: spec.tags.clone(),
            preview,
            sha256: hex_digest(&canonical),
            share_code: share_code::encode(spec)?,
        })
    }

    /// Decode, integrity-check and schema-validate the embedded crosshair.
    pub fn to_spec(&self) -> Result<CrosshairSpec, CatalogError> {
        let spec = share_code::decode(&self.share_code)?;
        if spec.id != self.id {
            return Err(CatalogError::Integrity(format!(
                "entry id {} does not match payload id {}",
                self.id, spec.id
            )));
        }
        let digest = hex_digest(&canonical_json(&spec)?);
        if digest != self.sha256 {
            return Err(CatalogError::Integrity(format!(
                "sha256 mismatch for {}",
                self.id
            )));
        }
        validate_spec(&spec).map_err(|errors| CatalogError::Invalid(errors.to_string()))?;
        Ok(spec)
    }

    /// Validate metadata that is used for display or file paths.
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), CatalogError> {
        if !is_safe_slug(&self.id) {
            return Err(CatalogError::Invalid(format!(
                "unsafe crosshair id: {}",
                self.id
            )));
        }
        if let Some(preview) = &self.preview {
            if !is_safe_relative_path(preview) {
                return Err(CatalogError::Invalid(format!(
                    "unsafe preview path: {preview}"
                )));
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// The published catalog document.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Catalog {
    #[serde(default = "default_schema_version")]
    pub schema_version: u32,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub generated_at: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub entries: Vec<CatalogEntry>,
}

fn default_schema_version() -> u32 {
    INDEX_SCHEMA_VERSION
}

impl Default for Catalog {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            schema_version: INDEX_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            generated_at: String::new(),
            entries: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

impl Catalog {
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), CatalogError> {
        if self.entries.len() > MAX_CATALOG_ENTRIES {
            return Err(CatalogError::Invalid(format!(
                "catalog has more than {MAX_CATALOG_ENTRIES} entries"
            )));
        }
        let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        for entry in &self.entries {
            entry.validate()?;
            if !seen.insert(entry.id.as_str()) {
                return Err(CatalogError::Invalid(format!(
                    "duplicate catalog id: {}",
                    entry.id
                )));
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn games(&self) -> Vec<GameTag> {
        let mut games: Vec<GameTag> = GameTag::ALL
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|game| self.entries.iter().any(|entry| entry.game == *game))
            .collect();
        games.sort_by_key(|game| game.label());
        games
    }

    pub fn by_id(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&CatalogEntry> {
        self.entries.iter().find(|entry| entry.id == id)
    }

    /// Case-insensitive substring search over name, author and tags.
    pub fn search<'a>(&'a self, query: &str) -> Vec<&'a CatalogEntry> {
        let needle = query.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
        if needle.is_empty() {
            return self.entries.iter().collect();
        }
        self.entries
            .iter()
            .filter(|entry| {
                entry.name.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle)
                    || entry.author.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle)
                    || entry
                        .tags
                        .iter()
                        .any(|tag| tag.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle))
                    || entry.id.contains(&needle)
            })
            .collect()
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum CatalogError {
    #[error("network error: {0}")]
    Network(String),
    #[error("catalog server returned HTTP {0}")]
    Status(u16),
    #[error("io error: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] io::Error),
    #[error("catalog JSON is invalid: {0}")]
    Json(#[from] serde_json::Error),
    #[error("share code error: {0}")]
    ShareCode(#[from] share_code::ShareCodeError),
    #[error("catalog entry is invalid: {0}")]
    Invalid(String),
    #[error("catalog entry failed its integrity check: {0}")]
    Integrity(String),
    #[error("no cached catalog is available")]
    NoCache,
}

/// Outcome of a catalog refresh.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SyncOutcome {
    pub catalog: Catalog,
    pub from_cache: bool,
    pub not_modified: bool,
    pub etag: Option<String>,
}

fn cache_path(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
    xhair_core::cache_dir().join(name)
}

/// The public index URL, honouring `XHAIR_INDEX_URL` for local testing.
pub fn index_url() -> String {
    std::env::var("XHAIR_INDEX_URL").unwrap_or_else(|_| DEFAULT_INDEX_URL.to_string())
}

fn read_cache() -> Result<Catalog, CatalogError> {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(cache_path(INDEX_FILE))?;
    let catalog: Catalog = serde_json::from_str(&text)?;
    catalog.validate()?;
    Ok(catalog)
}

fn read_etag() -> Option<String> {
    fs::read_to_string(cache_path(ETAG_FILE))
        .ok()
        .map(|value| value.trim().to_string())
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
}

fn write_atomically(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    }
    let temporary = path.with_extension("tmp");
    fs::write(&temporary, bytes)?;
    fs::rename(&temporary, path)?;
    Ok(())
}

fn write_cache(catalog: &Catalog, etag: Option<&str>) -> io::Result<()> {
    let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(catalog)?;
    write_atomically(&cache_path(INDEX_FILE), &bytes)?;
    if let Some(etag) = etag {
        write_atomically(&cache_path(ETAG_FILE), etag.as_bytes())?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Load the last synced catalog without touching the network.
pub fn load_cached() -> Result<Catalog, CatalogError> {
    read_cache().map_err(|error| match error {
        CatalogError::Io(_) => CatalogError::NoCache,
        other => other,
    })
}

/// Refresh the catalog from `url`, using the cached ETag when possible.
///
/// If the network is unavailable the last good cache is returned with
/// `from_cache = true`. A `304 Not Modified` keeps the cache and reports
/// `not_modified = true`.
pub fn sync(url: &str) -> Result<SyncOutcome, CatalogError> {
    let etag = read_etag();
    let agent = ureq::AgentBuilder::new()
        .timeout_connect(Duration::from_secs(8))
        .timeout_read(Duration::from_secs(15))
        .user_agent(USER_AGENT)
        .build();

    let mut request = agent.get(url);
    if let Some(etag) = &etag {
        request = request.set("If-None-Match", etag);
    }

    match request.call() {
        Ok(response) => {
            let new_etag = response
                .header("ETag")
                .map(|value| value.trim().to_string())
                .filter(|value| !value.is_empty());
            let mut body = Vec::new();
            response
                .into_reader()
                .take(MAX_INDEX_BYTES + 1)
                .read_to_end(&mut body)
                .map_err(|error| CatalogError::Network(error.to_string()))?;
            if body.len() as u64 > MAX_INDEX_BYTES {
                return Err(CatalogError::Network(
                    "catalog response exceeded the size limit".to_string(),
                ));
            }
            let catalog: Catalog = serde_json::from_slice(&body)?;
            catalog.validate()?;
            // Best-effort caching: a read-only cache directory must not break sync.
            let _ = write_cache(&catalog, new_etag.as_deref());
            Ok(SyncOutcome {
                catalog,
                from_cache: false,
                not_modified: false,
                etag: new_etag,
            })
        }
        Err(ureq::Error::Status(304, _)) => {
            let catalog = read_cache().map_err(|_| CatalogError::NoCache)?;
            Ok(SyncOutcome {
                catalog,
                from_cache: true,
                not_modified: true,
                etag,
            })
        }
        Err(ureq::Error::Status(status, _)) => {
            if let Ok(catalog) = read_cache() {
                Ok(SyncOutcome {
                    catalog,
                    from_cache: true,
                    not_modified: false,
                    etag,
                })
            } else {
                Err(CatalogError::Status(status))
            }
        }
        Err(ureq::Error::Transport(error)) => match read_cache() {
            Ok(catalog) => Ok(SyncOutcome {
                catalog,
                from_cache: true,
                not_modified: false,
                etag,
            }),
            Err(_) => Err(CatalogError::Network(error.to_string())),
        },
    }
}

/// Sync against the default (or overridden) public index.
pub fn sync_default() -> Result<SyncOutcome, CatalogError> {
    sync(&index_url())
}

/// GitHub issue URL prefilled with a share code for a community submission.
pub fn submission_url(spec: &CrosshairSpec) -> Result<String, CatalogError> {
    let code = share_code::encode(spec)?;
    let title = format!("[crosshair] {} ({})", spec.name, spec.game.label());
    let body = format!(
        "### Crosshair submission\n\n\
         **Name:** {}\n\
         **Game:** {}\n\
         **Author:** {}\n\n\
         Paste this share code into X-Hair Studio → Designer → Import to try it:\n\n\
         ```\n{code}\n```\n\n\
         - [ ] I created this crosshair and agree to license it under CC0-1.0.\n",
        spec.name,
        spec.game.label(),
        spec.author
    );
    Ok(format!(
        "https://github.com/{REPO_OWNER}/{REPO_NAME}/issues/new?template=crosshair-submission.yml&title={}&labels=community-submission&body={}",
        percent_encoding::utf8_percent_encode(&title, percent_encoding::NON_ALPHANUMERIC),
        percent_encoding::utf8_percent_encode(&body, percent_encoding::NON_ALPHANUMERIC)
    ))
}

/// GitHub repository URL for the community hub.
pub fn repository_url() -> String {
    format!("https://github.com/{REPO_OWNER}/{REPO_NAME}")
}

/// Join a catalog-relative path onto a trusted root, rejecting traversal.
pub fn safe_join(root: &Path, relative: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, CatalogError> {
    if !is_safe_relative_path(relative) {
        return Err(CatalogError::Invalid(format!(
            "unsafe relative path: {relative}"
        )));
    }
    Ok(root.join(relative))
}

/// Kebab-case identifier check compatible with the crosshair schema.
pub fn is_safe_slug(value: &str) -> bool {
    let bytes = value.as_bytes();
    !bytes.is_empty()
        && bytes.len() <= 64
        && bytes.first() != Some(&b'-')
        && bytes.last() != Some(&b'-')
        && !value.contains("--")
        && bytes
            .iter()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_lowercase() || byte.is_ascii_digit() || *byte == b'-')
}

/// True when `path` is relative, uses `/` separators and contains no `..`.
pub fn is_safe_relative_path(path: &str) -> bool {
    !path.is_empty()
        && !path.starts_with('/')
        && !path.contains('\\')
        && !path.starts_with("..")
        && path
            .split('/')
            .all(|part| !part.is_empty() && part != "." && part != "..")
}

fn canonical_json(spec: &CrosshairSpec) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CatalogError> {
    Ok(serde_json::to_vec(spec)?)
}

fn hex_digest(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
    hasher.update(bytes);
    let digest = hasher.finalize();
    let mut hex = String::with_capacity(digest.len() * 2);
    for byte in digest {
        use std::fmt::Write as _;
        let _ = write!(hex, "{byte:02x}");
    }
    hex
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use xhair_core::presets;

    #[test]
    fn entry_round_trips_and_validates() {
        let spec = presets::by_id("valorant-tenz").unwrap();
        let entry =
            CatalogEntry::from_spec(&spec, Some("previews/valorant/valorant-tenz.png".into()))
                .unwrap();
        entry.validate().unwrap();
        let decoded = entry.to_spec().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(decoded, spec);
    }

    #[test]
    fn tampered_payload_is_rejected() {
        let spec = presets::by_id("cs2-s1mple").unwrap();
        let mut entry = CatalogEntry::from_spec(&spec, None).unwrap();
        entry.sha256 = "0".repeat(64);
        assert!(matches!(entry.to_spec(), Err(CatalogError::Integrity(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn traversal_paths_are_rejected() {
        assert!(!is_safe_relative_path("../etc/passwd"));
        assert!(!is_safe_relative_path("/etc/passwd"));
        assert!(!is_safe_relative_path("a/../../b"));
        assert!(is_safe_relative_path("previews/valorant/tenz.png"));
        assert!(safe_join(Path::new("/tmp/root"), "../escape").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn slug_rules_match_schema() {
        assert!(is_safe_slug("valorant-tenz"));
        assert!(!is_safe_slug("Valorant Tenz"));
        assert!(!is_safe_slug("-bad"));
        assert!(!is_safe_slug("double--dash"));
        assert!(!is_safe_slug(""));
    }

    #[test]
    fn catalog_search_and_filters() {
        let mut catalog = Catalog::default();
        for id in ["valorant-tenz", "cs2-s1mple"] {
            let spec = presets::by_id(id).unwrap();
            catalog
                .entries
                .push(CatalogEntry::from_spec(&spec, None).unwrap());
        }
        assert_eq!(catalog.search("tenz").len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(catalog.search("").len(), 2);
        assert!(catalog.by_id("cs2-s1mple").is_some());
        assert!(catalog.games().contains(&GameTag::Valorant));

        let duplicate = catalog.entries[0].clone();
        catalog.entries.push(duplicate);
        assert!(catalog.validate().is_err());
    }
}
```

```rs
//! TOML configuration persistence.

use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use directories::ProjectDirs;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use thiserror::Error;

use crate::profile::GameProfile;

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum ConfigError {
    #[error("could not determine a configuration directory")]
    NoConfigDir,
    #[error("io error: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] io::Error),
    #[error("toml decode error: {0}")]
    Decode(#[from] toml::de::Error),
    #[error("toml encode error: {0}")]
    Encode(#[from] toml::ser::Error),
}

fn project_dirs() -> Option<ProjectDirs> {
    ProjectDirs::from("io", "xhair", "X-Hair")
}

/// Directory containing `config.toml`.
pub fn config_dir() -> PathBuf {
    project_dirs()
        .map(|dirs| dirs.config_dir().to_path_buf())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(".xhair"))
}

/// Directory used for the community ETag cache.
pub fn cache_dir() -> PathBuf {
    project_dirs()
        .map(|dirs| dirs.cache_dir().to_path_buf())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(".xhair/cache"))
}

pub fn config_path() -> PathBuf {
    config_dir().join("config.toml")
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct XHairConfig {
    pub active_profile: String,
    pub overlay_visible: bool,
    pub keep_above_fullscreen: bool,
    pub start_with_os: bool,
    pub notifications: bool,
    pub last_crosshair: String,
    pub favorites: Vec<String>,
    pub recent: Vec<String>,
    pub profiles: Vec<GameProfile>,
}

impl Default for XHairConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            active_profile: "Fortnite Tactical".to_string(),
            overlay_visible: true,
            keep_above_fullscreen: true,
            start_with_os: false,
            notifications: true,
            last_crosshair: "fortnite-bugha".to_string(),
            favorites: Vec::new(),
            recent: Vec::new(),
            profiles: GameProfile::builtin(),
        }
    }
}

impl XHairConfig {
    pub fn load() -> Result<Self, ConfigError> {
        Self::load_from(&config_path())
    }

    pub fn load_from(path: &Path) -> Result<Self, ConfigError> {
        if !path.exists() {
            return Ok(Self::default());
        }
        let text = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        Ok(toml::from_str(&text)?)
    }

    pub fn save(&self) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {
        self.save_to(&config_path())
    }

    pub fn save_to(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        let text = toml::to_string_pretty(self)?;
        let temporary = path.with_extension("toml.tmp");
        fs::write(&temporary, text)?;
        fs::rename(&temporary, path)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn active_game_profile(&self) -> Option<&GameProfile> {
        self.profiles
            .iter()
            .find(|profile| profile.name == self.active_profile)
            .or_else(|| self.profiles.first())
    }

    pub fn active_game_profile_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut GameProfile> {
        let name = self.active_profile.clone();
        if let Some(index) = self.profiles.iter().position(|p| p.name == name) {
            Some(&mut self.profiles[index])
        } else {
            self.profiles.first_mut()
        }
    }

    pub fn push_recent(&mut self, id: &str) {
        self.recent.retain(|existing| existing != id);
        self.recent.insert(0, id.to_string());
        self.recent.truncate(10);
    }

    /// Returns the new favorite state.
    pub fn toggle_favorite(&mut self, id: &str) -> bool {
        if let Some(index) = self.favorites.iter().position(|existing| existing == id) {
            self.favorites.remove(index);
            false
        } else {
            self.favorites.push(id.to_string());
            true
        }
    }

    pub fn is_favorite(&self, id: &str) -> bool {
        self.favorites.iter().any(|existing| existing == id)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn temp_path(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("xhair-test-{}-{}", std::process::id(), name));
        let _ = fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
        dir.join("config.toml")
    }

    #[test]
    fn defaults_are_sane() {
        let config = XHairConfig::default();
        assert!(config.overlay_visible);
        assert_eq!(config.profiles.len(), 4);
        assert_eq!(
            config.active_game_profile().unwrap().name,
            "Fortnite Tactical"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn round_trips_through_toml() {
        let path = temp_path("roundtrip");
        let mut config = XHairConfig::default();
        config.push_recent("valorant-tenz");
        config.push_recent("cs2-s1mple");
        config.push_recent("valorant-tenz");
        assert_eq!(config.recent, vec!["valorant-tenz", "cs2-s1mple"]);
        assert!(config.toggle_favorite("valorant-tenz"));
        config.save_to(&path).unwrap();

        let loaded = XHairConfig::load_from(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(loaded.favorites, vec!["valorant-tenz"]);
        assert_eq!(loaded.recent, config.recent);
        assert_eq!(loaded.profiles.len(), 4);
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap());
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_file_uses_defaults() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join("xhair-does-not-exist/config.toml");
        let config = XHairConfig::load_from(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(config.active_profile, "Fortnite Tactical");
    }
}
```

```rs
//! Core data model, presets, profiles and configuration for X-Hair.
//!
//! This crate is intentionally free of windowing, rendering and networking
//! dependencies so it can be reused by the overlay, the CLI and the community
//! pipeline.

pub mod config;
pub mod presets;
pub mod profile;
pub mod schema;
pub mod share_code;
pub mod validate;

pub use config::{cache_dir, config_dir, config_path, ConfigError, XHairConfig};
pub use profile::{
    profile_for_process, AdsBehavior, BindingMode, FireBehavior, GameProfile, HotkeyCombo, KeyCode,
    Modifier, MouseButton, MouseTriggerConfig, SlotBinding, Trigger,
};
pub use schema::{
    BloomConfig, BracketStyle, CrosshairCategory, CrosshairSpec, DotShape, GameTag, ReticleLayer,
    Rgba, TShape,
};
pub use share_code::{decode, encode, ShareCodeError, PREFIX as SHARE_CODE_PREFIX};
pub use validate::{validate_json, validate_spec, ValidationErrors, BUILTIN_SCHEMA};

/// Version of the crosshair schema described by [`CrosshairSpec`].
pub const SCHEMA_VERSION: u8 = 1;

/// Convenience map from preset id to spec for the whole built-in library.
pub fn library() -> std::collections::BTreeMap<String, CrosshairSpec> {
    presets::all_presets()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|spec| (spec.id.clone(), spec))
        .collect()
}
```

`crates/xhair-core/src/presets/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! The 100 built-in professional and tactical crosshairs.

mod apex;
mod cs2;
mod fortnite;
mod tactical_other;
mod valorant;
mod warzone;

use crate::schema::{
    BracketStyle, CrosshairCategory, CrosshairSpec, DotShape, GameTag, ReticleLayer, Rgba, TShape,
};

/// Every built-in preset, in stable library order.
pub fn all_presets() -> Vec<CrosshairSpec> {
    let mut presets = Vec::with_capacity(100);
    presets.extend(valorant::presets());
    presets.extend(cs2::presets());
    presets.extend(fortnite::presets());
    presets.extend(apex::presets());
    presets.extend(warzone::presets());
    presets.extend(tactical_other::presets());
    presets
}

/// Look up a built-in preset by its stable id.
pub fn by_id(id: &str) -> Option<CrosshairSpec> {
    all_presets().into_iter().find(|spec| spec.id == id)
}

/// All built-in presets for one game.
pub fn by_game(game: GameTag) -> Vec<CrosshairSpec> {
    all_presets()
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|spec| spec.game == game)
        .collect()
}

pub(crate) fn p(
    id: &str,
    name: &str,
    author: &str,
    game: GameTag,
    category: CrosshairCategory,
    tags: &[&str],
    layers: Vec<ReticleLayer>,
) -> CrosshairSpec {
    CrosshairSpec::new(id, name, author, game, category)
        .with_tags(tags)
        .with_layers(layers)
}

pub(crate) fn lines(length: f32, thickness: f32, gap: f32, color: Rgba) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::lines(
        length,
        thickness,
        gap,
        0.0,
        color,
        0.0,
        Rgba::BLACK,
        TShape::Never,
        false,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn lines_o(
    length: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    gap: f32,
    color: Rgba,
    outline: f32,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::lines(
        length,
        thickness,
        gap,
        0.0,
        color,
        outline,
        Rgba::BLACK,
        TShape::Never,
        false,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn lines_oo(
    length: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    gap: f32,
    color: Rgba,
    outline: f32,
    outline_color: Rgba,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::lines(
        length,
        thickness,
        gap,
        0.0,
        color,
        outline,
        outline_color,
        TShape::Never,
        false,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn lines_round(length: f32, thickness: f32, gap: f32, color: Rgba) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::lines(
        length,
        thickness,
        gap,
        0.0,
        color,
        0.0,
        Rgba::BLACK,
        TShape::Never,
        true,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn lines_t(
    length: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    gap: f32,
    color: Rgba,
    outline: f32,
    t_shape: TShape,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::lines(
        length,
        thickness,
        gap,
        0.0,
        color,
        outline,
        Rgba::BLACK,
        t_shape,
        false,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn dot(radius: f32, color: Rgba) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::center_dot(radius, DotShape::Circle, color, 0.0, Rgba::BLACK)
}

pub(crate) fn dot_o(radius: f32, color: Rgba, outline: f32) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::center_dot(radius, DotShape::Circle, color, outline, Rgba::BLACK)
}

pub(crate) fn dot_full(
    radius: f32,
    shape: DotShape,
    color: Rgba,
    outline: f32,
    outline_color: Rgba,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::center_dot(radius, shape, color, outline, outline_color)
}

pub(crate) fn ring(
    radius: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    color: Rgba,
    segments: u8,
    arc_gap: f32,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::circle_ring(
        radius,
        thickness,
        color,
        0.0,
        Rgba::BLACK,
        segments,
        arc_gap,
        0.0,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn ring_o(
    radius: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    color: Rgba,
    outline: f32,
    segments: u8,
    arc_gap: f32,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::circle_ring(
        radius,
        thickness,
        color,
        outline,
        Rgba::BLACK,
        segments,
        arc_gap,
        0.0,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn brackets(
    distance: f32,
    arm: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    style: BracketStyle,
    color: Rgba,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::brackets(distance, arm, thickness, style, color, 0.0, Rgba::BLACK)
}

pub(crate) fn brackets_o(
    distance: f32,
    arm: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    style: BracketStyle,
    color: Rgba,
    outline: f32,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::brackets(distance, arm, thickness, style, color, outline, Rgba::BLACK)
}

pub(crate) fn x_o(
    length: f32,
    thickness: f32,
    gap: f32,
    color: Rgba,
    outline: f32,
) -> ReticleLayer {
    ReticleLayer::diagonal_x(length, thickness, gap, color, outline, Rgba::BLACK)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::collections::HashSet;

    #[test]
    fn exactly_one_hundred_presets() {
        assert_eq!(all_presets().len(), 100);
    }

    #[test]
    fn preset_ids_are_unique() {
        let presets = all_presets();
        let mut seen = HashSet::new();
        for spec in &presets {
            assert!(
                seen.insert(spec.id.clone()),
                "duplicate preset id: {}",
                spec.id
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_game_roster_is_complete() {
        let counts = [
            (GameTag::Valorant, 20),
            (GameTag::Cs2, 20),
            (GameTag::Fortnite, 20),
            (GameTag::Apex, 15),
            (GameTag::Warzone, 10),
            (GameTag::R6Siege, 4),
            (GameTag::Overwatch2, 4),
            (GameTag::Universal, 7),
        ];
        for (game, expected) in counts {
            assert_eq!(
                by_game(game).len(),
                expected,
                "wrong roster size for {game:?}"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_preset_has_layers() {
        for spec in all_presets() {
            assert!(!spec.layers.is_empty(), "{} has no layers", spec.id);
            assert!(
                (16..=256).contains(&spec.canvas_size),
                "{} has an invalid canvas",
                spec.id
            );
        }
    }
}

```

`crates/xhair-core/src/presets/tactical_other.rs`:

```rs
//! Rainbow Six Siege, Overwatch 2 and universal tactical reticles.

use super::*;
use crate::schema::{BracketStyle, CrosshairCategory as C, DotShape, GameTag as G, Rgba};

const TURQUOISE: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(0, 230, 200);

pub(super) fn presets() -> Vec<CrosshairSpec> {
    vec![
        p(
            "r6-shaiiko",
            "Shaiiko",
            "Shaiiko",
            G::R6Siege,
            C::Pro,
            &["turquoise", "pixel-peek", "dot"],
            vec![lines(2.2, 1.1, 0.8, TURQUOISE), dot(0.9, TURQUOISE)],
        ),
```

`crates/xhair-core/src/profile.rs`:

```rs
//! Per-game profiles: weapon-slot bindings, ADS behavior and hotkeys.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::schema::Rgba;

/// A keyboard key identified by a stable name (`Digit1`, `A`, `F1`, …).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(transparent)]
pub struct KeyCode(pub String);

impl KeyCode {
    pub fn new(name: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        KeyCode(name.into())
    }

    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &str {
        &self.0
    }
}

impl From<&str> for KeyCode {
    fn from(value: &str) -> Self {
        KeyCode(value.to_string())
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Display for KeyCode {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(&self.0)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum MouseButton {
    Left,
    Right,
    Middle,
    Button4,
    Button5,
}

impl MouseButton {
    pub const fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            MouseButton::Left => "Left",
            MouseButton::Right => "Right",
            MouseButton::Middle => "Middle",
            MouseButton::Button4 => "Mouse4",
            MouseButton::Button5 => "Mouse5",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum Modifier {
    Alt,
    Shift,
    Ctrl,
    Super,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct HotkeyCombo {
    pub modifiers: Vec<Modifier>,
    pub key: KeyCode,
}

impl HotkeyCombo {
    pub fn new(modifiers: &[Modifier], key: impl Into<KeyCode>) -> Self {
        Self {
            modifiers: modifiers.to_vec(),
            key: key.into(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for HotkeyCombo {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(&[Modifier::Alt, Modifier::Shift], "X")
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum Trigger {
    Key(KeyCode),
    Mouse(MouseButton),
    WheelUp,
    WheelDown,
}

impl Trigger {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            Trigger::Key(key) => key.to_string(),
            Trigger::Mouse(button) => button.label().to_string(),
            Trigger::WheelUp => "Wheel Up".to_string(),
            Trigger::WheelDown => "Wheel Down".to_string(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum BindingMode {
    Switch,
    Hide,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SlotBinding {
    pub trigger: Trigger,
    pub crosshair_id: Option<String>,
    pub mode: BindingMode,
}

impl SlotBinding {
    pub fn switch(trigger: Trigger, crosshair_id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            trigger,
            crosshair_id: Some(crosshair_id.into()),
            mode: BindingMode::Switch,
        }
    }

    pub fn hide(trigger: Trigger) -> Self {
        Self {
            trigger,
            crosshair_id: None,
            mode: BindingMode::Hide,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum AdsBehavior {
    #[default]
    None,
    HideOnHold,
    SwitchCrosshair(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum FireBehavior {
    #[default]
    None,
    Bloom,
    HideTopArm,
    FlashColor(Rgba),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MouseTriggerConfig {
    pub ads_button: MouseButton,
    pub fire_button: MouseButton,
    pub switch_buttons: Vec<MouseButton>,
}

impl Default for MouseTriggerConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            ads_button: MouseButton::Right,
            fire_button: MouseButton::Left,
            switch_buttons: vec![MouseButton::Button4, MouseButton::Button5],
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GameProfile {
    pub name: String,
    pub process_names: Vec<String>,
    pub default_crosshair_id: String,
    pub slot_bindings: Vec<SlotBinding>,
    pub ads_behavior: AdsBehavior,
    pub fire_behavior: FireBehavior,
    pub build_mode_keys: Vec<KeyCode>,
    pub master_toggle_hotkey: HotkeyCombo,
    pub mouse_trigger: MouseTriggerConfig,
}

impl GameProfile {
    pub fn matches_process(&self, process: &str) -> bool {
        let process = process.to_ascii_lowercase();
        self.process_names
            .iter()
            .any(|name| name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&process))
    }

    pub fn binding_for(&self, trigger: &Trigger) -> Option<&SlotBinding> {
        self.slot_bindings
            .iter()
            .find(|binding| &binding.trigger == trigger)
    }

    /// The four profiles shipped out of the box.
    pub fn builtin() -> Vec<GameProfile> {
        vec![
            GameProfile {
                name: "Fortnite Tactical".to_string(),
                process_names: vec![
                    "FortniteClient-Win64-Shipping.exe".to_string(),
                    "FortniteClient-Win64-Shipping_EAC.exe".to_string(),
                ],
                default_crosshair_id: "fortnite-bugha".to_string(),
                slot_bindings: vec![
                    SlotBinding::switch(
                        Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit1")),
                        "universal-ads-micro-dot",
                    ),
                    SlotBinding::switch(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit2")), "fortnite-bugha"),
                    SlotBinding::switch(
                        Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit3")),
                        "fortnite-tactical-shotgun",
                    ),
                    SlotBinding::switch(
                        Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit4")),
                        "fortnite-tactical-smg-ring",
                    ),
                    SlotBinding::switch(
                        Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit5")),
                        "fortnite-tactical-sniper-mildot",
                    ),
                ],
                ads_behavior: AdsBehavior::SwitchCrosshair("universal-ads-micro-dot".to_string()),
                fire_behavior: FireBehavior::Bloom,
                build_mode_keys: vec![
                    KeyCode::from("F1"),
                    KeyCode::from("F2"),
                    KeyCode::from("F3"),
                    KeyCode::from("F4"),
                ],
                master_toggle_hotkey: HotkeyCombo::default(),
                mouse_trigger: MouseTriggerConfig::default(),
            },
            GameProfile {
                name: "Valorant Competitive".to_string(),
                process_names: vec!["VALORANT-Win64-Shipping.exe".to_string()],
                default_crosshair_id: "valorant-tenz".to_string(),
                slot_bindings: vec![
                    SlotBinding::switch(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit1")), "valorant-tenz"),
                    SlotBinding::switch(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit2")), "valorant-scream"),
                    SlotBinding::hide(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit3"))),
                ],
                ads_behavior: AdsBehavior::None,
                fire_behavior: FireBehavior::None,
                build_mode_keys: Vec::new(),
                master_toggle_hotkey: HotkeyCombo::default(),
                mouse_trigger: MouseTriggerConfig::default(),
            },
            GameProfile {
                name: "CS2 Competitive".to_string(),
                process_names: vec!["cs2.exe".to_string()],
                default_crosshair_id: "cs2-s1mple".to_string(),
                slot_bindings: vec![
                    SlotBinding::switch(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit1")), "cs2-s1mple"),
                    SlotBinding::switch(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit2")), "cs2-niko"),
                    SlotBinding::hide(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit3"))),
                ],
                ads_behavior: AdsBehavior::None,
                fire_behavior: FireBehavior::None,
                build_mode_keys: Vec::new(),
                master_toggle_hotkey: HotkeyCombo::default(),
                mouse_trigger: MouseTriggerConfig::default(),
            },
            GameProfile {
                name: "Apex Legends".to_string(),
                process_names: vec!["r5apex.exe".to_string(), "r5apex_dx12.exe".to_string()],
                default_crosshair_id: "apex-imperialhal".to_string(),
                slot_bindings: vec![
                    SlotBinding::switch(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit1")), "apex-imperialhal"),
                    SlotBinding::switch(Trigger::Key(KeyCode::from("Digit2")), "apex-imperialhal"),
                    SlotBinding::switch(
                        Trigger::Mouse(MouseButton::Button4),
                        "fortnite-tactical-shotgun",
                    ),
                ],
                ads_behavior: AdsBehavior::HideOnHold,
                fire_behavior: FireBehavior::None,
                build_mode_keys: Vec::new(),
                master_toggle_hotkey: HotkeyCombo::default(),
                mouse_trigger: MouseTriggerConfig::default(),
            },
        ]
    }

    pub fn default_profile() -> GameProfile {
        GameProfile::builtin()
            .into_iter()
            .next()
            .expect("at least one built-in profile")
    }
}

impl Default for GameProfile {
    fn default() -> Self {
        GameProfile::default_profile()
    }
}

/// Find the profile whose process matcher covers `process`.
pub fn profile_for_process<'a>(
    profiles: &'a [GameProfile],
    process: &str,
) -> Option<&'a GameProfile> {
    profiles
        .iter()
        .find(|profile| profile.matches_process(process))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::presets;

    #[test]
    fn every_profile_references_real_presets() {
        let ids: std::collections::HashSet<_> = presets::all_presets()
            .into_iter()
            .map(|spec| spec.id)
            .collect();
        for profile in GameProfile::builtin() {
            assert!(
                ids.contains(&profile.default_crosshair_id),
                "{} default id is missing",
                profile.name
            );
            for binding in &profile.slot_bindings {
                if let Some(id) = &binding.crosshair_id {
                    assert!(ids.contains(id), "{} references unknown {id}", profile.name);
                }
            }
            if let AdsBehavior::SwitchCrosshair(id) = &profile.ads_behavior {
                assert!(
                    ids.contains(id),
                    "{} ADS references unknown {id}",
                    profile.name
                );
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn process_matching_is_case_insensitive() {
        let profiles = GameProfile::builtin();
        assert!(profile_for_process(&profiles, "CS2.EXE").is_some());
        assert!(profile_for_process(&profiles, "notepad.exe").is_none());
    }
}
```

```rs
//! Crosshair data model shared by the overlay, renderer, CLI and community tooling.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// 8-bit sRGB color with straight (non-premultiplied) alpha.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Rgba {
    pub r: u8,
    pub g: u8,
    pub b: u8,
    pub a: u8,
}

impl Rgba {
    pub const TRANSPARENT: Rgba = Rgba::new(0, 0, 0, 0);
    pub const WHITE: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(255, 255, 255);
    pub const BLACK: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(0, 0, 0);
    pub const CYAN: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(0, 240, 255);
    pub const GREEN: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(0, 255, 0);
    pub const YELLOW: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(255, 230, 0);
    pub const RED: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(255, 48, 48);
    pub const ORANGE: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(255, 77, 0);
    pub const MAGENTA: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(255, 0, 255);
    pub const LIME: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(170, 255, 0);
    pub const BLUE: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(0, 170, 255);
    pub const PINK: Rgba = Rgba::rgb(255, 64, 180);

    pub const fn new(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8, a: u8) -> Self {
        Self { r, g, b, a }
    }

    pub const fn rgb(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8) -> Self {
        Self { r, g, b, a: 255 }
    }

    pub const fn with_alpha(self, a: u8) -> Self {
        Self { a, ..self }
    }

    pub const fn to_array(self) -> [u8; 4] {
        [self.r, self.g, self.b, self.a]
    }
}

impl Default for Rgba {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Rgba::WHITE
    }
}

/// A single composable drawing primitive. Layers are painted bottom-to-top.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum ReticleLayer {
    Lines {
        length: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        gap: f32,
        rotation_deg: f32,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
        t_shape: TShape,
        rounded_caps: bool,
    },
    CenterDot {
        radius: f32,
        shape: DotShape,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
    },
    CircleRing {
        radius: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
        segments: u8,
        arc_gap_deg: f32,
        rotation_deg: f32,
    },
    Brackets {
        distance: f32,
        arm_length: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        style: BracketStyle,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
    },
    DiagonalX {
        length: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        gap: f32,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
    },
    CustomImage {
        base64_png_or_svg: String,
        scale: f32,
        tint_color: Option<Rgba>,
    },
}

impl ReticleLayer {
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn lines(
        length: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        gap: f32,
        rotation_deg: f32,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
        t_shape: TShape,
        rounded_caps: bool,
    ) -> Self {
        ReticleLayer::Lines {
            length,
            thickness,
            gap,
            rotation_deg,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
            t_shape,
            rounded_caps,
        }
    }

    pub fn center_dot(
        radius: f32,
        shape: DotShape,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
    ) -> Self {
        ReticleLayer::CenterDot {
            radius,
            shape,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
        }
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn circle_ring(
        radius: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
        segments: u8,
        arc_gap_deg: f32,
        rotation_deg: f32,
    ) -> Self {
        ReticleLayer::CircleRing {
            radius,
            thickness,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
            segments,
            arc_gap_deg,
            rotation_deg,
        }
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn brackets(
        distance: f32,
        arm_length: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        style: BracketStyle,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
    ) -> Self {
        ReticleLayer::Brackets {
            distance,
            arm_length,
            thickness,
            style,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
        }
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn diagonal_x(
        length: f32,
        thickness: f32,
        gap: f32,
        color: Rgba,
        outline_thickness: f32,
        outline_color: Rgba,
    ) -> Self {
        ReticleLayer::DiagonalX {
            length,
            thickness,
            gap,
            color,
            outline_thickness,
            outline_color,
        }
    }

    pub fn custom_image(base64_png_or_svg: impl Into<String>, scale: f32) -> Self {
        ReticleLayer::CustomImage {
            base64_png_or_svg: base64_png_or_svg.into(),
            scale,
            tint_color: None,
        }
    }
}

/// Dynamic spread applied while the fire button is held.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BloomConfig {
    pub trigger_ms: u32,
    pub max_scale: f32,
    pub expand_px: f32,
    pub fade_ms: u32,
}

impl Default for BloomConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            trigger_ms: 120,
            max_scale: 1.6,
            expand_px: 6.0,
            fade_ms: 150,
        }
    }

/// The complete, portable description of one crosshair.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CrosshairSpec {
    pub schema_version: u8,
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub author: String,
    pub game: GameTag,
    pub category: CrosshairCategory,
    pub tags: Vec<String>,
    pub canvas_size: u32,
    pub offset_x: i32,
    pub offset_y: i32,
    pub global_opacity: f32,
    pub layers: Vec<ReticleLayer>,
    pub bloom: Option<BloomConfig>,
}

impl CrosshairSpec {
    pub fn new(
        id: impl Into<String>,
        name: impl Into<String>,
        author: impl Into<String>,
        game: GameTag,
        category: CrosshairCategory,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            schema_version: 1,
            id: id.into(),
            name: name.into(),
            author: author.into(),
            game,
            category,
            tags: Vec::new(),
            canvas_size: 128,
            offset_x: 0,
            offset_y: 0,
            global_opacity: 1.0,
            layers: Vec::new(),
            bloom: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_tags(mut self, tags: &[&str]) -> Self {
        self.tags = tags.iter().map(|t| (*t).to_string()).collect();
        self
    }

    pub fn with_layers(mut self, layers: Vec<ReticleLayer>) -> Self {
        self.layers = layers;
        self
    }

    pub fn with_bloom(mut self, bloom: BloomConfig) -> Self {
        self.bloom = Some(bloom);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_canvas(mut self, size: u32) -> Self {
        self.canvas_size = size;
        self
    }
}
```

`crates/xhair-core/src/share_code.rs`:

```rs
//! Compact `XHAIR1:` share codes: base64url(deflate(json)).

use std::io::{Read, Write};

use base64::engine::general_purpose::URL_SAFE_NO_PAD;
use base64::Engine as _;
use flate2::read::DeflateDecoder;
use flate2::write::DeflateEncoder;
use flate2::Compression;
use thiserror::Error;

use crate::schema::CrosshairSpec;

pub const PREFIX: &str = "XHAIR1:";

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum ShareCodeError {
    #[error("share code is missing the {PREFIX} prefix")]
    MissingPrefix,
    #[error("share code is not valid base64url: {0}")]
    Base64(#[from] base64::DecodeError),
    #[error("share code payload is not a valid deflate stream: {0}")]
    Inflate(#[from] std::io::Error),
    #[error("share code JSON is invalid: {0}")]
    Json(#[from] serde_json::Error),
}

/// Encode a crosshair into a compact, URL-safe share string.
pub fn encode(spec: &CrosshairSpec) -> Result<String, ShareCodeError> {
    let json = serde_json::to_vec(spec)?;
    let mut encoder = DeflateEncoder::new(Vec::new(), Compression::best());
    encoder.write_all(&json)?;
    let compressed = encoder.finish()?;
    Ok(format!("{PREFIX}{}", URL_SAFE_NO_PAD.encode(compressed)))
}

/// Decode a share string produced by [`encode`].
pub fn decode(code: &str) -> Result<CrosshairSpec, ShareCodeError> {
    let payload = code
        .trim()
        .strip_prefix(PREFIX)
        .ok_or(ShareCodeError::MissingPrefix)?;
    let compressed = URL_SAFE_NO_PAD.decode(payload)?;
    let mut decoder = DeflateDecoder::new(compressed.as_slice());
    let mut json = Vec::new();
    decoder.read_to_end(&mut json)?;
    Ok(serde_json::from_slice(&json)?)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::presets;

    #[test]
    fn prefix_is_stable() {
        assert!(encode(&presets::all_presets()[0])
            .unwrap()
            .starts_with(PREFIX));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_foreign_prefix() {
        assert!(matches!(
            decode("NOPE:123"),
            Err(ShareCodeError::MissingPrefix)
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn round_trips_every_builtin_preset() {
        for spec in presets::all_presets() {
            let code = encode(&spec).unwrap();
            let decoded = decode(&code).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(
                spec, decoded,
                "share code round-trip failed for {}",
                spec.id
            );
        }
    }
}
```

`crates/xhair-core/src/validate.rs`:

```rs
//! A small, dependency-light JSON Schema (2020-12 subset) validator.
//!
//! The community schema only needs object/array/number/string constraints,
//! enums, patterns, ranges and `oneOf`; implementing that subset keeps the
//! binary lean while still validating every contributed crosshair against the
//! canonical [`community/schema/crosshair.schema.json`] file.

use std::fmt;

use regex::Regex;
use serde_json::Value;

use crate::schema::CrosshairSpec;

/// Canonical schema shipped with the repository.
pub const BUILTIN_SCHEMA: &str = include_str!("../../../community/schema/crosshair.schema.json");

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ValidationErrors(pub Vec<String>);

impl fmt::Display for ValidationErrors {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        for (i, error) in self.0.iter().enumerate() {
            if i > 0 {
                writeln!(f)?;
            }
            write!(f, "- {error}")?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ValidationErrors {}

/// Validate a concrete crosshair against the built-in schema.
pub fn validate_spec(spec: &CrosshairSpec) -> Result<(), ValidationErrors> {
    let schema: Value = serde_json::from_str(BUILTIN_SCHEMA)
        .map_err(|e| ValidationErrors(vec![format!("built-in schema is invalid JSON: {e}")]))?;
    let instance = serde_json::to_value(spec)
        .map_err(|e| ValidationErrors(vec![format!("crosshair does not serialize: {e}")]))?;
    validate_instance(&schema, &instance)
}

/// Validate any JSON document against any schema string.
pub fn validate_json(schema_json: &str, instance: &Value) -> Result<(), ValidationErrors> {
    let schema: Value = serde_json::from_str(schema_json)
        .map_err(|e| ValidationErrors(vec![format!("schema is invalid JSON: {e}")]))?;
    validate_instance(&schema, instance)
}

/// Validate a JSON instance against a parsed schema value.
pub fn validate_instance(schema: &Value, instance: &Value) -> Result<(), ValidationErrors> {
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    check(schema, schema, instance, "$", &mut errors);
    if errors.is_empty() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(ValidationErrors(errors))
    }
}

fn json_type(value: &Value) -> &'static str {
    match value {
        Value::Null => "null",
        Value::Bool(_) => "boolean",
        Value::Number(n) => {
            if n.is_i64() || n.is_u64() {
                "integer"
            } else {
                "number"
            }
        }
        Value::String(_) => "string",
        Value::Array(_) => "array",
        Value::Object(_) => "object",
    }
}

fn type_matches(expected: &str, value: &Value) -> bool {
    match expected {
        "integer" => matches!(value, Value::Number(n) if n.is_i64() || n.is_u64()),
        "number" => matches!(value, Value::Number(_)),
        other => json_type(value) == other,
    }
}

fn resolve<'a>(root: &'a Value, reference: &str) -> Option<&'a Value> {
    let pointer = reference.strip_prefix('#')?;
    root.pointer(pointer)
}

fn check(schema: &Value, root: &Value, instance: &Value, path: &str, errors: &mut Vec<String>) {
    match schema {
        Value::Bool(true) => return,
        Value::Bool(false) => {
            errors.push(format!("{path}: no value is allowed here"));
            return;
        }
        Value::Object(_) => {}
        _ => {
            errors.push(format!("{path}: schema fragment is not an object"));
            return;
        }
    }

    if let Some(reference) = schema.get("$ref").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        match resolve(root, reference) {
            Some(target) => check(target, root, instance, path, errors),
            None => errors.push(format!("{path}: unresolvable $ref {reference}")),
        }
        return;
    }

    if let Some(expected) = schema.get("type") {
        let ok = match expected {
            Value::String(t) => type_matches(t, instance),
            Value::Array(types) => types
                .iter()
                .filter_map(Value::as_str)
                .any(|t| type_matches(t, instance)),
            _ => true,
        };
        if !ok {
            errors.push(format!(
                "{path}: expected type {expected}, found {}",
                json_type(instance)
            ));
            return;
        }
    }

    if let Some(Value::Array(options)) = schema.get("enum") {
        if !options.iter().any(|option| option == instance) {
            errors.push(format!(
                "{path}: value {instance} is not one of {options:?}"
            ));
        }
    }

    if let Some(constant) = schema.get("const") {
        if constant != instance {
            errors.push(format!(
                "{path}: expected constant {constant}, found {instance}"
            ));
        }
    }

    if let Some(pattern) = schema.get("pattern").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        if let Value::String(text) = instance {
            match Regex::new(pattern) {
                Ok(re) if !re.is_match(text) => errors.push(format!(
                    "{path}: \"{text}\" does not match pattern {pattern}"
                )),
                Err(err) => errors.push(format!("{path}: invalid schema pattern: {err}")),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }

    if let Value::String(text) = instance {
        check_len(
            schema,
            "minLength",
            text.chars().count(),
            path,
            errors,
            |a, b| a >= b,
        );
        check_len(
            schema,
            "maxLength",
            text.chars().count(),
            path,
            errors,
            |a, b| a <= b,
        );
    }

    if let Value::Array(items) = instance {
        if let Some(min) = schema.get("minItems").and_then(Value::as_u64) {
            if (items.len() as u64) < min {
                errors.push(format!("{path}: needs at least {min} item(s)"));
            }
        }
        if let Some(max) = schema.get("maxItems").and_then(Value::as_u64) {
            if (items.len() as u64) > max {
                errors.push(format!("{path}: allows at most {max} item(s)"));
            }
        }
        if let Some(item_schema) = schema.get("items") {
            for (index, item) in items.iter().enumerate() {
                check(item_schema, root, item, &format!("{path}[{index}]"), errors);
            }
        }
    }

    if let Value::Number(number) = instance {
        if let Some(min) = schema.get("minimum").and_then(Value::as_f64) {
            if number.as_f64().is_some_and(|n| n < min) {
                errors.push(format!("{path}: must be >= {min}"));
            }
        }
        if let Some(max) = schema.get("maximum").and_then(Value::as_f64) {
            if number.as_f64().is_some_and(|n| n > max) {
                errors.push(format!("{path}: must be <= {max}"));
            }
        }
        if let Some(min) = schema.get("exclusiveMinimum").and_then(Value::as_f64) {
            if number.as_f64().is_some_and(|n| n <= min) {
                errors.push(format!("{path}: must be > {min}"));
            }
        }
        if let Some(max) = schema.get("exclusiveMaximum").and_then(Value::as_f64) {
            if number.as_f64().is_some_and(|n| n >= max) {
                errors.push(format!("{path}: must be < {max}"));
            }
        }
    }

    if let Value::Object(object) = instance {
        if let Some(required) = schema.get("required").and_then(Value::as_array) {
            for key in required.iter().filter_map(Value::as_str) {
                if !object.contains_key(key) {
                    errors.push(format!("{path}: missing required property \"{key}\""));
                }
            }
        }

        let properties = schema.get("properties").and_then(Value::as_object);
        if let Some(properties) = properties {
            for (key, value) in object {
                if let Some(property_schema) = properties.get(key) {
                    check(
                        property_schema,
                        root,
                        value,
                        &format!("{path}.{key}"),
                        errors,
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        match schema.get("additionalProperties") {
            Some(Value::Bool(false)) => {
                if let Some(properties) = properties {
                    for key in object.keys() {
                        if !properties.contains_key(key) {
                            errors.push(format!(
                                "{path}: additional property \"{key}\" is not allowed"
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            Some(extra_schema @ Value::Object(_)) => {
                if let Some(properties) = properties {
                    for (key, value) in object {
                        if !properties.contains_key(key) {
                            check(extra_schema, root, value, &format!("{path}.{key}"), errors);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    if let Some(branches) = schema.get("oneOf").and_then(Value::as_array) {
        let mut matched = 0;
        let mut details = Vec::new();
        for branch in branches {
            let mut branch_errors = Vec::new();
            check(branch, root, instance, path, &mut branch_errors);
            if branch_errors.is_empty() {
                matched += 1;
            } else {
                details.push(branch_errors.join("; "));
            }
        }
        if matched != 1 {
            errors.push(format!(
                "{path}: expected exactly one matching oneOf branch, matched {matched}"
            ));
            if std::env::var_os("XHAIR_SCHEMA_TRACE").is_some() {
                errors.extend(details);
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(branches) = schema.get("anyOf").and_then(Value::as_array) {
        let any = branches.iter().any(|branch| {
            let mut branch_errors = Vec::new();
            check(branch, root, instance, path, &mut branch_errors);
            branch_errors.is_empty()
        });
        if !any {
            errors.push(format!("{path}: no anyOf branch matched"));
        }
    }

    if let Some(branches) = schema.get("allOf").and_then(Value::as_array) {
        for branch in branches {
            check(branch, root, instance, path, errors);
        }
    }
}

fn check_len(
    schema: &Value,
    key: &str,
    actual: usize,
    path: &str,
    errors: &mut Vec<String>,
    predicate: impl Fn(usize, usize) -> bool,
) {
    if let Some(limit) = schema.get(key).and_then(Value::as_u64) {
        if !predicate(actual, limit as usize) {
            errors.push(format!("{path}: {key} is {limit}, actual is {actual}"));
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::presets;

    #[test]
    fn builtin_schema_parses() {
        let parsed: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(BUILTIN_SCHEMA).expect("schema must be valid JSON");
        assert_eq!(
            parsed["$schema"],
            "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_builtin_preset_matches_schema() {
        for spec in presets::all_presets() {
            validate_spec(&spec).unwrap_or_else(|errors| {
                panic!("preset {} failed schema validation:\n{errors}", spec.id)
            });
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_bad_ids() {
        let mut spec = presets::all_presets()[0].clone();
        spec.id = "Not Kebab Case!".into();
        assert!(validate_spec(&spec).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_out_of_range_canvas() {
        let mut spec = presets::all_presets()[0].clone();
        spec.canvas_size = 1024;
        assert!(validate_spec(&spec).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unknown_layer_type() {
        let mut spec = presets::all_presets()[0].clone();
        spec.layers[0] = crate::schema::ReticleLayer::center_dot(
            1.0,
            crate::schema::DotShape::Circle,
            crate::schema::Rgba::WHITE,
            0.0,
            crate::schema::Rgba::BLACK,
        );
        assert!(validate_spec(&spec).is_ok());
    }
```

`crates/xhair-platform/src/lib.rs`:

```rs
//! Native platform backends for X-Hair.
//!
//! Everything here is OS-native and deliberately hook-free: layered/ARGB
//! windows for the overlay, Raw Input or evdev for weapon-slot switching, and
//! window-manager queries for active-game detection.

use crossbeam_channel::Sender;
use thiserror::Error;
use xhair_core::{KeyCode, MouseButton};

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub mod linux;
#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
pub mod windows;

/// One rendered crosshair frame, ready for the OS compositor.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct OverlayFrame {
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub offset_x: i32,
    pub offset_y: i32,
    /// Premultiplied BGRA, row-major, `width * height * 4` bytes.
    pub bgra_premultiplied: Vec<u8>,
}

impl OverlayFrame {
    pub fn new(width: u32, height: u32, offset_x: i32, offset_y: i32, bgra: Vec<u8>) -> Self {
        Self {
            width,
            height,
            offset_x,
            offset_y,
            bgra_premultiplied: bgra,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SessionKind {
    Windows,
    X11,
    Wayland,
    Unknown,
}

impl SessionKind {
    pub const fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            SessionKind::Windows => "Windows",
            SessionKind::X11 => "X11",
            SessionKind::Wayland => "Wayland",
            SessionKind::Unknown => "Unknown",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum InputEvent {
    KeyDown(KeyCode),
    KeyUp(KeyCode),
    MouseDown(MouseButton),
    MouseUp(MouseButton),
    Wheel(i32),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct WindowEvent {
    pub process: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum PlatformError {
    #[error("this platform backend is not available: {0}")]
    Unsupported(String),
    #[error("backend error: {0}")]
    Backend(String),
    #[error("missing permission: {0}")]
    Permission(String),
    #[error("io error: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
}

pub type PlatformResult<T> = Result<T, PlatformError>;

/// A transparent, click-through, always-on-top crosshair surface.
pub trait OverlayBackend {
    /// Render `frame` and show the overlay if it was hidden.
    fn show(&mut self, frame: &OverlayFrame) -> PlatformResult<()>;
    /// Repaint an already visible overlay.
    fn update(&mut self, frame: &OverlayFrame) -> PlatformResult<()>;
    fn hide(&mut self) -> PlatformResult<()>;
    fn is_visible(&self) -> bool;
    /// Re-assert topmost z-order when games switch to borderless fullscreen.
    fn set_keep_above(&mut self, enabled: bool) -> PlatformResult<()>;
}

/// Passive global keyboard/mouse listener. Never grabs or injects input.
pub trait InputListener: Send {
    fn start(&mut self, tx: Sender<InputEvent>) -> PlatformResult<()>;
    fn stop(&mut self);
    /// Human-readable fix if global input is unavailable (e.g. Linux groups).
    fn permission_hint(&self) -> Option<String>;
}

/// Watches the foreground application without polling the game.
pub trait WindowWatcher: Send {
    fn start(&mut self, tx: Sender<WindowEvent>) -> PlatformResult<()>;
    fn stop(&mut self);
    fn active_process(&self) -> Option<String>;
}

/// Platform-specific startup configuration (DPI awareness on Windows).
pub fn configure_process() {
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    windows::configure_process_dpi();
}

/// What kind of session we are running in.
pub fn session_kind() -> SessionKind {
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    {
        SessionKind::Windows
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        linux::session_kind()
    }
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
    {
        SessionKind::Unknown
    }
}

/// Create the best available overlay backend for this session.
pub fn create_overlay() -> PlatformResult<Box<dyn OverlayBackend>> {
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    {
        Ok(Box::new(windows::overlay::WindowsOverlay::new()?))
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        linux::create_overlay()
    }
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
    {
        Err(PlatformError::Unsupported(std::env::consts::OS.to_string()))
    }
}

/// Create the passive input listener for this platform.
pub fn create_input_listener() -> Box<dyn InputListener> {
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    {
        Box::new(windows::input::WindowsInputListener::new())
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        linux::create_input_listener()
    }
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
    {
        Box::new(UnsupportedInputListener)
    }
}

/// Create the active-window watcher for this platform.
pub fn create_window_watcher() -> Box<dyn WindowWatcher> {
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    {
        Box::new(windows::active_window::WindowsWindowWatcher::new())
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        linux::create_window_watcher()
    }
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
    {
        Box::new(UnsupportedWindowWatcher)
    }
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
struct UnsupportedInputListener;

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
impl InputListener for UnsupportedInputListener {
    fn start(&mut self, _tx: Sender<InputEvent>) -> PlatformResult<()> {
        Err(PlatformError::Unsupported(std::env::consts::OS.to_string()))
    }
    fn stop(&mut self) {}
    fn permission_hint(&self) -> Option<String> {
        None
    }
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
struct UnsupportedWindowWatcher;

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "linux")))]
impl WindowWatcher for UnsupportedWindowWatcher {
    fn start(&mut self, _tx: Sender<WindowEvent>) -> PlatformResult<()> {
        Err(PlatformError::Unsupported(std::env::consts::OS.to_string()))
    }
    fn stop(&mut self) {}
    fn active_process(&self) -> Option<String> {
        None
    }
}
```

`crates/xhair-platform/src/linux/active_window.rs`:

```rs
//! Foreground process detection: `_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW` on X11, explicit
//! fallback on Wayland (compositors do not expose a standard global query).

use std::fs;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::thread::JoinHandle;
use std::time::Duration;

use crossbeam_channel::Sender;
use x11rb::connection::Connection;
use x11rb::protocol::xproto::{AtomEnum, ConnectionExt as _};

use crate::{PlatformResult, SessionKind, WindowEvent, WindowWatcher};

/// Best-effort active process name for the current Linux session.
pub fn active_process(session: SessionKind) -> Option<String> {
    match session {
        SessionKind::X11 => x11_active_process(),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn x11_active_process() -> Option<String> {
    let (conn, screen_num) = x11rb::connect(None).ok()?;
    let root = conn.setup().roots[screen_num].root;
    let active_atom = conn
        .intern_atom(false, b"_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW")
        .ok()?
        .reply()
        .ok()?
        .atom;
    let property = conn
        .get_property(false, root, active_atom, AtomEnum::WINDOW, 0, 1)
        .ok()?
        .reply()
        .ok()?;
    let window = property.value32().and_then(|mut values| values.next())?;
    if window == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    let pid_atom = conn
        .intern_atom(false, b"_NET_WM_PID")
        .ok()?
        .reply()
        .ok()?
        .atom;
    let pid_property = conn
        .get_property(false, window, pid_atom, AtomEnum::CARDINAL, 0, 1)
        .ok()?
        .reply()
        .ok()?;
    let pid = pid_property
        .value32()
        .and_then(|mut values| values.next())?;
    process_name_from_pid(pid)
}

fn process_name_from_pid(pid: u32) -> Option<String> {
    if let Ok(path) = fs::read_link(format!("/proc/{pid}/exe")) {
        if let Some(name) = path.file_name() {
            return Some(name.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        }
    }
    fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/comm"))
        .ok()
        .map(|name| name.trim().to_string())
        .filter(|name| !name.is_empty())
}

pub struct X11WindowWatcher {
    stop: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    last: Arc<Mutex<Option<String>>>,
    thread: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
}

impl X11WindowWatcher {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            stop: Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false)),
            last: Arc::new(Mutex::new(None)),
            thread: None,
        }
    }
}

impl Default for X11WindowWatcher {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl WindowWatcher for X11WindowWatcher {
    fn start(&mut self, tx: Sender<WindowEvent>) -> PlatformResult<()> {
        if self.thread.is_some() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let stop = Arc::clone(&self.stop);
        let last = Arc::clone(&self.last);
        self.thread = Some(std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut previous: Option<Option<String>> = None;
            while !stop.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                let current = x11_active_process();
                if previous.as_ref() != Some(&current) {
                    if let Ok(mut slot) = last.lock() {
                        *slot = current.clone();
                    }
                    let _ = tx.try_send(WindowEvent {
                        process: current.clone(),
                    });
                    previous = Some(current);
                }
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300));
            }
        }));
        Ok(())
    }

    fn stop(&mut self) {
        self.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.thread = None;
    }

    fn active_process(&self) -> Option<String> {
        self.last.lock().ok().and_then(|slot| slot.clone())
    }
}

pub struct WaylandWindowWatcher {
    stop: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    thread: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
}

impl WaylandWindowWatcher {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            stop: Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false)),
            thread: None,
        }
    }
}

impl Default for WaylandWindowWatcher {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl WindowWatcher for WaylandWindowWatcher {
    fn start(&mut self, tx: Sender<WindowEvent>) -> PlatformResult<()> {
        if self.thread.is_some() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let stop = Arc::clone(&self.stop);
        self.thread = Some(std::thread::spawn(move || {
            // Layer-shell compositors do not expose a portable foreground
            // process query. Emit one explicit "unknown" so profiles fall back
            // to the user-selected default.
            let _ = tx.try_send(WindowEvent { process: None });
            while !stop.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1000));
            }
        }));
        Ok(())
    }

    fn stop(&mut self) {
        self.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.thread = None;
    }

    fn active_process(&self) -> Option<String> {
        None
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn resolves_our_own_process_name() {
        let name = process_name_from_pid(std::process::id());
        assert!(name.is_some());
        assert!(!name.unwrap().is_empty());
    }
}
```
